| Muscle | Origin | Insertion | Innervation | Function |
|--------|--------|-----------|-------------|----------|
| **Trapezius** | Superior nuchal line, external occipital protuberance, medial margin of ligamentum nuchae, spinous processes of CVII to TXII and related supraspinous ligaments | Superior edge of crest of spine of scapula, acromion, posterior border of lateral one-third of clavicle | Motor spinal part of accessory nerve (XI). Sensory (proprioception) anterior rami of C3 and C4 | Powerful elevator of the scapula; rotates the scapula during abduction of humerus above horizontal; middle fibers retract scapula; lower fibers depress scapula |
| **Deltoid** | Inferior edge of crest of spine of scapula, lateral margin of acromion, anterior border of lateral one-third of clavicle | Deltoid tuberosity of humerus | Axillary nerve (C5, C6) | Major abductor of arm (abducts arm beyond initial 15° done by supraspinatus); clavicular fibers assist in flexing the arm; posterior fibers assist in extending the arm |
| **Levator scapulae** | Transverse processes of C1 and C2 vertebrae and posterior tubercles of transverse processes of CIII and CIV vertebrae | Posterior surface of medial border of scapula from superior angle to root of spine of the scapula | Branches directly from anterior rami of C3 and (4) spinal nerves and by branches (C5) from the dorsal scapular nerve | Elevates the scapula |
| **Rhomboid minor** | Lower end of ligamentum nuchae and spinous processes of CVII and TI vertebrae | Posterior surface of medial border of scapula at the root of the spine of the scapula | Dorsal scapular nerve (C4, C5) | Elevates and retracts the scapula |
| **Rhomboid major** | Spinous processes of TII–TV vertebrae and intervening supraspinous ligaments | Posterior surface of medial border of scapula from the root of the spine of the scapula to the inferior angle | Dorsal scapular nerve (C4, C5) | Elevates and retracts the scapula |

---

### Table 7.2 — Muscles of the Posterior Scapular Region

| Muscle | Origin | Insertion | Innervation | Function |
|--------|--------|-----------|-------------|----------|
| **Supraspinatus** | Medial two-thirds of the supraspinous fossa of the scapula and the deep fascia that covers the muscle | Most superior facet on the greater tubercle of the humerus | Suprascapular nerve (C5, C6) | Rotator cuff muscle; initiation of abduction of arm to 15° at glenohumeral joint |
| **Infraspinatus** | Medial two-thirds of the infraspinous fossa of the scapula and the deep fascia that covers the muscle | Middle facet on posterior surface of the greater tubercle of the humerus | Suprascapular nerve (C5, C6) | Rotator cuff muscle; lateral rotation of arm at glenohumeral joint |
| **Teres minor** | Upper two-thirds of a flattened strip of bone on the posterior surface of the scapula immediately adjacent to the lateral border of the scapula | Inferior facet on the posterior surface of the greater tubercle of the humerus | Axillary nerve (C5, C6) | Rotator cuff muscle; lateral rotation of arm at glenohumeral joint |
| **Teres major** | Elongate oval area on the posterior surface of the inferior angle of the scapula | Medial lip of the intertubercular sulcus on the anterior surface of the humerus | Inferior subscapular nerve (C5, C6, C7) | Medial rotation and extension of the arm at the glenohumeral joint |
| **Long head of triceps brachii** | Infraglenoid tubercle on scapula | Common tendon of insertion with medial and lateral heads on the olecranon process of ulna | Radial nerve (C6, C7, C8) | Extension of the forearm at the elbow joint; accessory adductor and extensor of the arm at the glenohumeral joint |

# Upper Limb — Posterior Scapular Region & Axilla

---

## Triangular Space（三角間隙）

The **triangular space** 是肩胛後區與腋窩之間的 comunication area（圖 7.37）。

從肩胛後區觀察，triangular space 的邊界為：

- **long head of triceps brachii**（肱三頭肌長頭）的**內側緣**
- **teres major**（大圓肌）的**上緣**
- **teres minor**（小圓肌）的**下緣**

**Circumflex scapular artery 與靜脈**通過此間隙（圖 7.38）。

---

## Triangular Interval（三角窗）

Triangular interval 的邊界為：

- **long head of triceps brachii**（肱三頭肌長頭）的**外側緣**
- **humerus**（肱骨）的**骨幹**
- **teres major**（大圓肌）的**下緣**（圖 7.37）

由於此空間低於界定腋窩下界的 **teres major 下緣**，因此 triangular interval 作為：
- 上臂**前、後筋膜室**之間的通道
- 上臂**後筋膜室**與**腋窩**之間的通道

**Radial nerve**、**profunda brachii artery**（上臂深动脉）及相關靜脈通過此處（圖 7.38）。

---

## Nerves（神經）

Posterior Scapular Region 的兩條主要神經為 **suprascapular nerve** 與 **axillary nerve**，兩者皆起源於腋窩中的 **brachial plexus**（臂神經叢）（圖 7.38）。

### Suprascapular Nerve

**Suprascapular nerve** 起源於頸根（base of neck），由 **brachial plexus 的上幹**發出。從起始處向後外側走行，穿過 **suprascapular foramen** 到達肩胛後區，介於骨與肌肉之間（圖 7.38）。

- 支配 **supraspinatus muscle**（岡上肌）
- 隨後穿過 **greater scapular (spinoglenoid) notch**（肩胛大切跡），位於肩胛棘根部與關節盂之間，終止並支配 **infraspinatus muscle**（岡下肌）

通常 **suprascapular nerve 無皮枝**。

### Axillary Nerve

**Axillary nerve** 起源於 **brachial plexus 的後束**。經由腋窩後壁的 **quadrangular space** 離開腋窩，進入肩胛後區（圖 7.38）。

- 與 **posterior circumflex humeral artery 與靜脈**伴行
- 與 **humerus surgical neck**（肱骨外科頸）的**後表面**直接相鄰

Axillary nerve 支配 **deltoid**（三角肌）與 **teres minor**（小圓肌）。此外，有一皮枝——**superior lateral cutaneous nerve of the arm**，支配三角肌下部表面的皮膚感覺。

---

## Arteries and Veins（動脈與靜脈）

Posterior Scapular Region 有三條主要 arteries：

- **Suprascapular artery**
- **Posterior circumflex humeral artery**
- **Circumflex scapular artery**

這些 arteries 在肩胛周圍形成互聯的**血管網絡**（圖 7.39）。

### Suprascapular Artery

**Suprascapular artery** 起源於頸根，為 **thyrocervical trunk**（甲狀頸幹）的分支——而 thyrocervical trunk 是 **subclavian artery**（鎖骨下動脈）的主要分支（圖 7.38、7.39）。此血管也可能直接起源於 **subclavian artery 的第三段**。

正常情況下，suprascapular artery 在 **suprascapular foramen 上方**進入肩胛後區，而神經則穿過此 foramen。在肩胛後區，血管與 **suprascapular nerve** 伴行。

除供應 **supraspinatus** 與 **infraspinatus** muscles 外，suprascapular artery 沿途還分出 branches 至多個結構。

### Posterior Circumflex Humeral Artery

**Posterior circumflex humeral artery** 起源於腋窩中 **axillary artery 的第三段**（圖 7.39）。

此 artery 與 **axillary nerve** 經由腋窩後壁的 **quadrangular space** 離開腋窩，進入肩胛後區。供應相關肌肉及 **glenohumeral joint**（盂肱關節）。

### Circumflex Scapular Artery

**Circumflex scapular artery** 為 **subscapular artery** 的分支，亦起源於腋窩中 **axillary artery 的第三段**（圖 7.39）。此 artery 經由 **triangular space** 離開腋窩，進入肩胛後區，穿過 **teres minor muscle** 的起始部，與區域內其他 arteries 形成**吻合連接**。

### Veins（靜脈）

Posterior scapular region 的靜脈大致伴行 arteries，並與頸部、背部、上臂及腋窩的血管相連。

> **臨床重點**：Quadrangular Space Syndrome（四邊隙症候群）
>
> **Quadrangular space**（四邊隙）肌肉肥大或肌肉邊緣纖維化可能壓迫 **axillary nerve**。少見情況下導致 **deltoid muscle** 無力。通常引起 **teres minor muscle** 萎縮，進而影響旋轉袖肌群對肩關節運動的控制。

---

## AXILLA（腋窩）

**Axilla** 是上肢的門戶，提供頸部與上臂之間的過渡區域（圖 7.40A）。由 **clavicle**（鎖骨）、**scapula**（肩胛骨）、**上胸壁**、**humerus** 及相關肌肉所形成的 irregilarly shaped pyramidal space（四邊不規則的錐體空間），具有：

- 四面牆（walls）
- 一個**入口（inlet）**
- 一個**底部（floor/base）**（圖 7.40A、B）

腋窩入口**上方**與頸部相續，底部**外側**向上臂開放。所有進出上肢的主要結構皆通過腋窩（圖 7.40C）。前、後壁肌肉之間形成的 apertures 使結構得以在腋窩與相鄰區域（肩胛後區、胸肌區、三角肌區）之間通行。

---

### Axillary Inlet（腋窩入口）

Axillary inlet 位於**水平面**，大致呈**三角形**，頂尖指向外側（圖 7.40A、B）。入口邊緣完全由**骨頭**構成：

- **內側緣**：第一肋骨（rib I）的**外側界**
- **前緣**：clavicle 的**後表面**
- **後緣**：肩胛上緣至**喙突**（coracoid process）

三角形腋窩入口的**頂尖**位於外側，由 **coracoid process** 的**內側面**構成。

主要血管與神經在頸部與腋窩之間通行時，需跨越 **rib I 的外側緣**並穿過腋窩入口（圖 7.40A）。

- **Subclavian artery**（鎖骨下動脈）在跨越 rib I 外側緣並進入腋窩後，延續為 **axillary artery**（腋窩動脈）
- **Axillary vein**（腋窩靜脈）在跨越 rib I 並離開腋窩進入頸部後，延續為 **subclavian vein**（鎖骨下靜脈）

在腋窩入口處，**axillary vein** 位於 **axillary artery** 的**前方**，而 artery 位於 **brachial plexus 神經幹**的**前方**。

Brachial plexus 的**下幹（inferior trunk）**位於頸部 rib I 的正上方，subclavian artery 與靜脈亦然。當它們跨越 rib I 時，靜脈與 artery 被 **anterior scalene muscle**（前斜角肌）的**插入點**分開（圖 7.40A）。

---

### Anterior Wall（前壁）

腋窩前壁由以下結構組成（表 7.3）：

- **Pectoralis major muscle**（胸大肌）
- **Pectoralis minor muscle**（胸小肌）
- **Subclavius muscle**（鎖骨下肌）
- **Clavipectoral fascia**（鎖胸筋膜）

#### Muscles of the Anterior Wall（前壁肌肉）

| Muscle | Origin | Insertion | Innervation | Function |
|--------|---------|-----------|--------------|----------|
| **Pectoralis major** | Clavicular head：鎖骨內側半的前表面；Sternocostal head：胸骨前表面、第一至第七肋軟骨、第六肋骨的胸骨端、外斜肌的腱膜 | 肱骨 intertubercular sulcus 的**外側唇** | Medial 與 Lateral pectoral nerves；Clavicular head（C5, C6）；Sternocostal head（C6, C7, C8, T1） | 在 glenohumeral joint 屈曲、內收、內旋上臂；Clavicular head：使伸直的上臂屈曲；Sternocostal head：使屈曲的上臂伸直 |
| **Subclavius** | 第一肋骨，位於肋骨與肋軟骨交界處 | 鎖骨中三分之一下表面的淺溝 | Nerve to subclavius（C5, C6） | 向下拉肩部尖端（壓低鎖骨）；向內拉鎖骨以穩定胸鎖關節 |
| **Pectoralis minor** | 第三至第五肋骨前表面及上緣；覆蓋相鄰肋間隙的深筋膜 | 肩胛骨 coracoid process（內側緣及上表面） | Medial pectoral nerve（C5, C6, C7, C8, T1） | 向下拉肩部尖端；向前拉肩胛骨 |

##### Pectoralis Major

**Pectoralis major** 是前壁最大、最淺層的肌肉（圖 7.41）。其下緣構成 **anterior axillary fold**（前腋皺襞），是腋窩的前下界。此肌肉有兩個頭：

- **Clavicular head**：起源於 clavicle 的內側半
- **Sternocostal head**：起源於胸廓前壁的內側部——通常此頭的纖維繼續向下、向內延伸，附著於腹壁，形成肌肉的**腹部部分**

肌肉插入肱骨 intertubercular sulcus 的**外側唇**。起源於軀幹上方的部分，插入位置較低且較前方；起源於下方的部分，插入位置較高。

兩頭共同作用時，pectralis major 在 glenohumeral joint 使上臂**屈曲、內收、內旋**。Clavicular head 使上臂從伸直位置屈曲；Sternocostal head 使上臂從屈曲位置伸直（尤其在抗阻力時）。

由起源於腋窩內 brachial plexus 的 **lateral 與 medial pectoral nerves** 支配。

##### Subclavius

**Subclavius** 是一塊位於 pectoralis major 深層的小肌肉，介於 clavicle 與第一肋骨之間（圖 7.42）。以肌腱形式從第一肋骨與肋軟骨交界處起始，向外側並向上走行，以肌性附著插入鎖骨中三分之一下表面的狹淺溝內。

功能尚不完全清楚，可能作用為：
- 通過壓低鎖骨向下拉肩部
- 通過向內拉鎖骨穩定胸鎖關節

由起源於 brachial plexus 上幹的小分支支配。

##### Pectoralis Minor

**Pectoralis minor** 是一塊位於 pectoralis major 深層的**三角形**肌肉，從胸壁延伸至肩胛骨的 coracoid process（圖 7.42）。以三個肌腹形式起源於第三至第五肋骨的前表面及上緣，以及覆蓋相鄰肋間隙的深筋膜。肌纖維向上、外側走行，插入 coracoid process 的**內側及上部**。

此肌肉**向前拉肩胛骨**（使肩胛骨在胸壁上向前滑動），並**壓低肩胛骨的外側角**。

由起源於腋窩內 brachial plexus 的 **medial pectoral nerve** 支配。

##### Clavipectoral Fascia（鎖胸筋膜）

**Clavipectoral fascia** 是一片厚的結締組織膜，連接 clavicle 與腋窩底部（圖 7.42）。包繞 **subclavius** 與 **pectoralis minor** muscles，並跨越兩者之間的間隙。

結構在腋窩與前壁之間通行時，需穿過 clavipectoral fascia，方式為：

- 在 **subclavius 與 pectoralis minor muscles 之間**，或
- 在 **pectoralis minor muscle 下方**

穿過 subclavius 與 pectoralis minor 之間的重要結構包括：**cephalic vein**（頭靜脈）、**thoraco-acromial artery**（胸肩峰動脈）、**lateral pectoral nerve**。

**Lateral thoracic artery** 通過 pectoralis minor 下方穿過筋膜離開腋窩。

**Medial pectoral nerve** 穿過 pectoralis minor 直接進入該肌肉以支配之，並到達 pectoralis major。偶爾，medial pectoral nerve 的分支繞過 pectoralis minor 的下緣到達並支配覆蓋其上的 pectoralis major。

---

### Medial Wall（內側壁）

腋窩內側壁由**上胸壁**（肋骨及相關肋間組織）與 **serratus anterior muscle**（前鋸肌）組成（圖 7.43、表 7.4）。

#### Serratus Anterior

**Serratus anterior** 以多個肌腹形式起源於第一至第九肋骨的**外側面**及覆蓋相鄰肋間隙的深筋膜（圖 7.43）。肌肉形成扁平 sheet，繞過胸壁向後延伸，主要附著於肩胛骨**內側緣的肋面**。

Serratus anterior 向前拉肩胛骨，使其在胸壁上滑動，並促進肩胛骨的**旋轉**。它亦使肩胛骨的肋面與胸壁緊密相貼。

由 **long thoracic nerve**（胸長神經）支配——此神經源自 brachial plexus 的根部，沿內側壁通過腋窩，沿 serratus anterior 的**外表面**垂直下行，位於皮膚與淺筋膜的深層。

#### Intercostobrachial Nerve

穿過內側壁直接進入腋窩的唯一主要結構是 **intercostobrachial nerve**（圖 7.43）。此神經是第二肋間神經的**外側皮支**（T2 的前支）。在腋窩內與 brachial plexus 的分支（**medial cutaneous nerve of the arm**）交通，供應上臂後內側的皮膚，屬於 **T2 皮節**。

> **臨床重點**：Scapular "Winging"（肩胛翼狀變形）
>
> 由於 **long thoracic nerve** 沿胸壁外側面下行於 serratus anterior 的外表面，位於皮膚與皮下筋膜的深層，因此容易受損。此肌肉功能喪失導致肩胛骨**內側緣**（尤其是**下角**）抬起離開胸壁，在上臂向前推時產生典型的肩胛骨「翼狀」外觀。此外，正常的上臂抬高動作也無法完成。

#### Muscles of the Medial Wall（內側壁肌肉）

| Muscle | Origin | Insertion | Innervation | Function |
|--------|---------|-----------|--------------|----------|
| **Serratus anterior** | 上 8-9 肋骨的外側面及覆蓋相鄰肋間隙的深筋膜 | 肩胛骨內側緣的肋面 | Long thoracic nerve（C5, C6, C7） | 拉肩胛骨向前並旋轉；使肩胛骨內側緣及下角與胸壁相貼 |

---

### Lateral Wall（外側壁）

腋窩外側壁**狹窄**，完全由 **humerus 的 intertubercular sulcus**（結節間溝）構成（圖 7.44）。

- 前壁的 **pectoralis major** 附著於 intertubercular sulcus 的**外側唇**
- 後壁的 **latissimus dorsi** 與 **teres major** 分別附著於 sulcus 的**底部**與**內側唇**（表 7.5）

---

### Posterior Wall（後壁）

腋窩後壁結構複雜（圖 7.45，另見圖 7.50）。其骨框架由肩胛骨的**肋面**構成。後壁的肌肉包括：

- **Subscapularis muscle**（肩胛下肌）：附著於肩胛骨的肋面
- **Latissimus dorsi** 與 **teres major** 的**遠端部分**：從背部及肩胛後區進入後壁
- **Long head of triceps brachii** 的**近端部分**：垂直下行穿過後壁進入上臂

後壁肌肉之間的間隙形成 apertures，結構得以在腋窩、肩胛後區與上臂後筋膜室之間通行。

#### Muscles of the Lateral and Posterior Walls（外側壁與後壁肌肉）

| Muscle | Origin | Insertion | Innervation | Function |
|--------|---------|-----------|--------------|----------|
| **Subscapularis** | Subscapular fossa 的內側三分之二 | 肱骨**小結節** | Upper 與 Lower subscapular nerves（C5, C6, (C7)） | 旋轉袖肌；在 glenohumeral joint 使上臂**內旋** |
| **Teres major** | 肩胛骨下角後表面的狹長橢圓形區域 | 肱骨前表面 intertubercular sulcus 的**內側唇** | Lower subscapular nerve（C5, C6, C7） | 在 glenohumeral joint 使上臂**內旋、伸直、內收** |
| **Latissimus dorsi** | 下六個胸椎的棘突及相關棘間韌帶；經胸腰筋膜至腰椎棘突、相關棘間韌帶及髂嵴；下 3-4 肋骨 | Intertubercular sulcus 的**底部** | Thoracodorsal nerve（C6, C7, C8） | 在 glenohumeral joint 使上臂**內收、內旋、伸直** |
| **Long head of triceps brachii** | 肩胛骨的 **infraglenoid tubercule** | 與內側頭、外側頭共同腱性附著於尺骨**鷹嘴突** | Radial nerve（C6, C7, C8） | 在肘關節伸直前臂；在 glenohumeral joint 輔助內收、伸直上臂 |

---

### Arterial Anastomoses Around the Shoulder（肩關節周圍的动脉吻合）

肩關節周圍的arterial anastomoses（动脉吻合）如圖 7.39 所示。

主要吻合 arterials 包括：

- **Suprascapular artery**
- **Subscapular artery**
- **Anterior circumflex humeral artery**
- **Posterior circumflex humeral artery**
- **Circumflex scapular artery**

這些 arteries 在肩胛骨周圍形成一個**互聯的血管網絡**，確保上肢的側支循環。


# Gray's Anatomy — Head & Neck
## Regional Anatomy — Axilla（腋窩）

---

## 後壁（Posterior Wall）

### Subscapularis Muscle（肩胛下肌）

**Subscapularis** 構成腋窩後壁的最大部分，起源於 **subscapular fossa（肩胛下窩）**，附著於 **humerus（肱骨）** 的 **lesser tubercle（小結節）**（圖 7.45 與 7.46）。肌腱於 **glenohumeral joint（盂肱關節）** 關節囊前方通過。

肩胛下肌與後肩胛區的三塊肌肉（**supraspinatus（棘上肌）**、**infraspinatus（棘下肌）**、**teres minor（小圓肌）**）同屬於 **rotator cuff（旋轉袖）** 肌群，穩定 **glenohumeral joint**。

肩胛下肌由臂神經叢分支（**superior subscapular nerve** 與 **inferior subscapular nerve**）支配，這些神經起源於腋窩。

---

### Teres Major（大圓肌）與 Latissimus Dorsi（背闊肌）

腋窩後壁的外下側由 **teres major muscle** 的終末部分與 **latissimus dorsi muscle** 的扁平腱膜構成（圖 7.45）。這兩個結構位於 **posterior axillary fold（後腋皺襞）** 下方，標誌著腋窩的後下界。

**Latissimus dorsi** 的扁平腱膜繞過 **teres major** 的下緣，於後壁向前附著於 **humerus** 的 **intertubercular sulcus（結節間溝）** 底部，位置略上方高於 **teres major** 附著於結節間溝內側唇的最遠端附著點。因此，**teres major** 的下緣定義了腋窩外側的下界。

**Axillary artery（腋動脈）** 在越過 **teres major** 下緣後延續為 **brachial artery（臂動脈）**。

---

### Long Head of Triceps Brachii（肱三頭肌長頭）

**Triceps brachii** 的長頭垂直穿過腋窩後壁，與周圍肌肉及相鄰骨頭共同形成三個孔隙，為主要結構通過後壁提供通道：

- **Quadrangular space（四邊形空間）**
- **Triangular space（三角形空間）**
- **Triangular interval（三角形間隙）**（圖 7.45）

---

## 後壁的通道（Gateways in the Posterior Wall）

> 另見「後肩胛區的通道」，第 717–721 頁，及圖 7.37 與 7.38。

### Quadrangular Space（四邊形空間）

四邊形空間為神經與血管在腋窩與更後方的肩胛區及三角肌區之間提供通道（圖 7.45）。從前方觀察，其邊界由以下結構形成：

- **Subscapularis muscle** 的下緣
- **Humerus** 的 **surgical neck（外科頸）**
- **Teres major muscle** 的上緣
- **Triceps brachii** 長頭的外側緣

通過四邊形空間的結構：**Axillary nerve（腋神經）** 與 **posterior circumflex humeral artery（旋肱後動脈）** 和靜脈。

### Triangular Space（三角形空間）

三角形空間是腋窩與後肩胛區之間的交通區域（圖 7.45）。從前方觀察，由以下結構形成：

- **Triceps brachii** 長頭的內側緣
- **Teres major muscle** 的上緣
- **Subscapularis muscle** 的下緣

**Circumflex scapular artery（旋肩胛動脈）** 與靜脈通過此空間。

### Triangular Interval（三角形間隙）

此三角形間隙由以下結構形成：

- **Triceps brachii** 長頭的外側緣
- **Humerus** 的骨幹（shaft）
- **Teres major muscle** 的下緣（圖 7.45）

**Radial nerve（橈神經）** 離開腋窩，經由此間隙進入臂的後間隔。

---

## 底（Floor）

腋窩的底由筋膜與一片拱形的皮膚構成，跨越各壁的下緣之間的距離（圖 7.47 並見圖 7.40B）。底由 **clavipectoral fascia（鎖胸筋膜）** 支撐。在患者身上，**anterior axillary fold（前腋皺襞）** 的位置比 **posterior axillary fold（後腋皺襞）** 更高。

下方，結構在底的外側進入和離開腋窩，此處前壁與後壁會合，腋窩與臂的前間隔相延續。

---

## 腋窩的內容物（Contents of the Axilla）

通過腋窩的是上肢的主要血管、神經與淋巴管。此空間也容納臂的兩塊肌肉的近端部分、乳腺的腋尾部及淋巴結群，引流上肢、胸壁與乳腺。

**Biceps brachii（肱二頭肌）** 與 **Coracobrachialis（喙肱肌）** 的近端部分通過腋窩（表 7.6）。

> **臨床重點**：腋窩淋巴結引流上肢、胸壁與乳腺的淋巴。乳腺癌時，腋窩淋巴結清除術是重要的分期與治療步驟。

---

### 表 7.6 通過腋窩的肌肉

| 肌肉 | 起源 | 附著 | 支配 | 功能 |
|------|------|------|------|------|
| **Biceps brachii** | 長頭：scapula 的 supraglenoid tubercle；短頭：coracoid process 的 apex | Radius 的 radial tuberosity | **Musculocutaneous nerve**（C5, C6） | 前臂於肘關節的強大屈肌與旋後肌；於 glenohumeral joint 的輔助屈臂肌 |
| **Coracobrachialis** | Coracoid process 的 apex | Humerus 中段的線性粗糙區 | **Musculocutaneous nerve**（C5, C6, C7） | 於 glenohumeral joint 的屈臂肌；於內側收臂 |

---

## 腋窩內容物：肌肉（Contents of the Axilla: Muscles）

### Biceps Brachii（肱二頭肌）

**Biceps brachii** 以兩個頭起源（圖 7.48）：

- **短頭** 起源於 **scapula** 的 **coracoid process（喙突）** apex，垂直通過腋窩進入臂部，與長頭會合。
- **長頭** 以肌腱起源於 **scapula** 的 **supraglenoid tubercle（盂上結節）**，於 **humerus** 頭部深方通過 **glenohumeral joint** 關節囊，進入 **intertubercular sulcus**，由 **transverse humeral ligament（橫肱韌帶）** 固定於大結節與小結節之間；肌腱通過腋窩的結節間溝，於臂近端形成肌腹。

肌肉的長頭與短頭於臂遠端會合，主要以單一肌腱附著於前臂的 **radial tuberosity（橈骨粗隆）**。

**Biceps brachii** 主要為前臂於肘關節的強大屈肌與前臂的強大旋後肌。因為兩個頭都起源於 scapula，該肌肉也是臂於 **glenohumeral joint** 的輔助屈肌。此外，長頭防止 humerus 於 glenoid cavity 向上移動。

**Biceps brachii** 由 **musculocutaneous nerve（肌皮神經）** 支配。

### Coracobrachialis（喙肱肌）

**Coracobrachialis** 與 **biceps brachii** 的短頭共同起源於 **coracoid process** 的 apex（圖 7.48）。它垂直通過腋窩，附著於 **humerus** 內側面大約中段的線性粗糙區。

**Coracobrachialis** 於 **glenohumeral joint** 屈臂。

在腋窩中，**coracobrachialis** 的內側面被 **musculocutaneous nerve** 穿過，該神經支配後通過肌肉進入臂部。

---

## 腋窩內容物：Axillary Artery（腋動脈）

**Axillary artery** 供應腋窩壁及相關區域，並作為上肢更遠端部分的主要血液供應（圖 7.49）。

頸部的 **subclavian artery（鎖骨下動脈）** 在 **rib I（第一肋）** 的外側緣變為 **axillary artery**，穿過腋窩，在 **teres major muscle** 的下緣延續為 **brachial artery**。

**Axillary artery** 被越過其前方的 **pectoralis minor muscle（胸小肌）** 分為三部分（圖 7.49）：

- **第一部分**：在 **pectoralis minor** 的近端
- **第二部分**：在 **pectoralis minor** 的後方
- **第三部分**：在 **pectoralis minor** 的遠端

一般而言，腋動脈有六條分支：

- **第一部分**：**Superior thoracic artery（胸上動脈）**
- **第二部分**：**Thoraco-acromial artery（胸肩峰動脈）** 與 **lateral thoracic artery（胸外側動脈）**
- **第三部分**：**Subscapular artery（肩胛下動脈）**、**anterior circumflex humeral artery（旋肱前動脈）** 與 **posterior circumflex humeral artery（旋肱後動脈）**（圖 7.50）

---

### Superior Thoracic Artery（胸上動脈）

**Superior thoracic artery** 較細，起源於 **axillary artery** 第一部分的前表面（圖 7.50）。供應內側與前腋窩壁上區域。

### Thoraco-Acromial Artery（胸肩峰動脈）

**Thoraco-acromial artery** 較短，起源於 **axillary artery** 第二部分的前表面，正好在 **pectoralis minor** 的內側（上方）緣後方（圖 7.50）。它繞過肌肉的上緣，穿過 **clavipectoral fascia（鎖胸筋膜）**，立即分為四個分支——**pectoral branch（胸肌支）**、**deltoid branch（三角肌支）**、**clavicular branch（鎖骨支）** 與 **acromial branch（肩峰支）**，供應前腋窩壁及相關區域。

此外，**pectoral branch** 為乳腺提供血液供應，**deltoid branch** 進入 **clavipectoral triangle（鎖胸三角）**，與 **cephalic vein（頭靜脈）** 伴行並供應相鄰結構（見圖 7.41）。

### Lateral Thoracic Artery（胸外側動脈）

**Lateral thoracic artery** 起源於 **axillary artery** 第二部分的前表面，在 **pectoralis minor** 的外側（下方）緣後方（圖 7.50）。它沿肌肉邊緣走向胸壁，供應腋窩的內側壁與前壁。在女性，分支從 **pectoralis major（胸大肌）** 下緣周圍走出，為乳腺的血液供應做出貢獻。

### Subscapular Artery（肩胛下動脈）

**Subscapular artery** 是 **axillary artery** 最大的分支，是腋窩後壁的主要血液供應（圖 7.50）。它也為後肩胛區的血液供應做出貢獻。

**Subscapular artery** 起源於 **axillary artery** 第三部分的後表面，沿 **subscapularis muscle** 的下緣短距離行走，然後分為兩個終支：**circumflex scapular artery** 與 **thoracodorsal artery（胸背動脈）**。

- **Circumflex scapular artery** 通過 **subscapularis**、**teres major** 與 **triceps** 長頭之間的三角形空間。後方，它從 **teres minor** 起源的下方穿過或穿過它進入 **infraspinous fossa（岡下窩）**。它與 **suprascapular artery（棘上動脈）** 及 **transverse cervical artery（頸橫動脈）** 的深支（dorsal scapular artery）吻合，為肩胛骨周圍的血管吻合網絡做出貢獻。
- **Thoracodorsal artery** 大致沿肩胛骨外側緣走向下角。它為腋窩後壁與內側壁的血液供應做出貢獻。

### Anterior Circumflex Humeral Artery（旋肱前動脈）

**Anterior circumflex humeral artery** 比 **posterior circumflex humeral artery** 細，起源於 **axillary artery** 第三部分的外側（圖 7.50）。它於 **humerus** 的 **surgical neck** 前方通過，與 **posterior circumflex humeral artery** 吻合。

此动脉為周圍組織（包括 **glenohumeral joint** 與 **humerus** 頭）供應分支。

### Posterior Circumflex Humeral Artery（旋肱後動脈）

**Posterior circumflex humeral artery** 起源於 **axillary artery** 第三部分的外側表面，正好在 **anterior circumflex humeral artery** 起點的後方（圖 7.50）。它與 **axillary nerve** 一起，通過 **teres major**、**teres minor**、**triceps brachii** 長頭與 **humerus** 的 **surgical neck** 之間的四邊形空間離開腋窩。

**Posterior circumflex humeral artery** 繞過 **humerus** 的 **surgical neck**，為周圍肌肉與 **glenohumeral joint** 供應。它與 **anterior circumflex humeral artery** 以及來自 **profunda brachii**、**suprascapular** 與 **thoraco-acromial arteries** 的分支吻合。

---

## Axillary Vein（腋靜脈）

**Axillary vein** 起於 **teres major muscle** 的下緣，是 **basilic vein（貴要靜脈）** 的延續（圖 7.51），貴要靜脈是引流手與前臂後內側面的淺靜脈，在臂中段穿透深筋膜。

**Axillary vein** 在腋窩中於 **axillary artery** 的內側與前方通過，在血管越過 **rib I** 外側緣的腋窩入口處延續為 **subclavian vein（鎖骨下靜脈）**。腋靜脈的屬支通常跟隨 **axillary artery** 的分支。其他屬支包括跟隨 **brachial artery** 的 **brachial veins** 與 **cephalic vein（頭靜脈）**。

**Cephalic vein** 是引流手部後外側與後部、前臂與臂部的淺靜脈。在肩部區域，它進入 **deltoid muscle**、**pectoralis major** 與 **clavicle** 之間的倒三角形裂隙（**clavipectoral triangle**）。在 **clavipectoral triangle** 的上部，**cephalic vein** 穿過 **pectoralis major** 的鎖骨頭深方，穿透 **clavipectoral fascia** 加入 **axillary vein**。

> **臨床重點**：許多病情危重的患者失血或失液，需要補充。靜脈通路是補充液體的必要手段。典型的靜脈通路部位是手部的 **cephalic vein** 或肘窩（cubital fossa）淺層組織中的靜脈。

---

## 臨床重點：上肢血液供應的影像學檢查

> **臨床重點**：當有臨床上血管受損的證據，或需要建立動靜脈瘻管（血液透析所需）時，需要影像學檢查來評估血管。

**超聲波** 是對上肢血管進行非侵入性評估的有用工具，可評估從 **subclavian artery** 第三部分到深部與淺部掌弓的血管。可以量化血流並記錄解剖變異。

**血管造影** 在某些情況下進行。於腹股溝韌帶下方穿刺股總動脈，將長導管經髂动脉繞過主動脈弓進入左鎖骨下动脉或頭臂幹再到右鎖骨下动脉。將不透射線的對比劑注入血管，在對比劑首先通過动脉、毛細血管最後到達靜脈時拍攝X光片。

---

## 臨床重點：上肢动脉外傷

> **臨床重點**：上肢的动脉供應在相對固定或皮下位置特別容易受到外傷。

### 第一肋骨骨折

當 **subclavian artery** 從頸部穿出進入腋窩時，被周圍肌肉固定於 **rib I** 的上表面。上胸部創傷導致的快速減速損傷可能造成第一肋骨骨折，可能嚴重影響 **subclavian artery** 的遠端部分或 **axillary artery** 的第一部分。幸運的是，**subclavian artery** 與 **axillary artery** 分支之間存在吻合連接，在肩胛骨與肱骨近端周圍形成血管網絡；因此，即使完全血管切斷，臂也很少完全缺血（缺血是指器官或肢體的血液供應不良）。

### 肱骨頭前脫位

**肱骨頭前脫位** 可能壓迫 **axillary artery**，導致血管閉塞。這不太可能使上肢完全缺血，但可能需要手術重建 **axillary artery** 以獲得無痛功能。重要的是，**axillary artery** 與 **brachial plexus（臂神經叢）** 密切相關，在前脫位時可能受損。

---

## 臨床重點：Subclavian/Axillary 靜脈通路

> **臨床重點**：有多种途径可以获得中心静脉通路。「锁骨下途径」和颈静脉途径是临床常用的方法。锁骨下途径这个名称虽然不够准确，但在临床实践中仍然是首选术语。事实上，大多数临床医生进入的是腋静脉的第一部分。

需要进行 **subclavian vein/axillary vein** 導管置入的患者有許多例。

進入 **subclavian vein/axillary vein** 是相對簡單的技術。識別鎖骨，在鎖骨下區放置銳利針頭，指向內上。當抽吸到靜脈血時，即獲得通路。此途徑常用於長期靜脈通路（如 Hickman 管）和短期通路（多種腔導管插入，如重症監護室）。

**Subclavian vein/axillary vein** 也是起搏器導線植入的首選部位。然而，有一個首選的進入點以防止併發症。應在鎖骨中線或此線的外側穿刺靜脈。這樣做的原因是靜脈的路徑及其與其他結構的關係。靜脈在走向胸廓入口時，位於动脉前方、第一肋上方、鎖骨下方。鎖骨下方有 **subclavius muscle（鎖骨下肌）**。如果穿刺部位正好在 **subclavius muscle** 與 **axillary vein** 相關之處，導管或導線可能在此處打折。而且，這塊肌肉持續的收縮與放鬆會導致管線疲勞，最終可能導致斷裂。斷裂的起搏器導線或化療導管破裂可能對患者造成嚴重後果。

---

## Brachial Plexus（臂神經叢）

**Brachial plexus** 是由 **C5** 至 **C8** 的前支與 **T1** 前支的大部分組成的軀體神經叢（圖 7.52）。神經叢起源於頸部，向外下越過 **rib I** 進入腋窩。

臂神經叢的各部分從內側到外側依次為：**roots（根）**、**trunks（幹）**、**divisions（股）** 與 **cords（束）**。所有支配上肢的主要神經都起源於臂神經叢，主要來自 cords。臂神經叢的近端部分在頸部位於 **subclavian artery** 後方，而更遠端部分圍繞 **axillary artery**。

臂神經叢的近端部分在頸部位於 **subclavian artery** 後方，而更遠端部分圍繞 **axillary artery**。

---

*圖 7.45–7.52：腋窩的區域解剖學。圖 7.45 顯示後壁的通道；圖 7.46 為磁共振横切面影像；圖 7.47 為腋窩底的示意圖；圖 7.48 為通過腋窩的肌肉；圖 7.49 為腋动脉及其與 pectoralis minor 的關係；圖 7.50 為腋动脉的分支；圖 7.51 為腋靜脈；圖 7.52 為臂神經叢。*

# Head & Neck — Brachial Plexus & Axilla

---

## Brachial Plexus（臂神經叢）

### Roots（神經根）

The roots of the **brachial plexus** are the anterior rami of **C5** to **C8**, and most of **T1**. Close to their origin, the roots receive **gray rami communicantes** from the **sympathetic trunk** (Fig. 7.52). These carry postganglionic sympathetic fibers onto the roots for distribution to the periphery. The roots and trunks enter the **posterior triangle of the neck** by passing between the **anterior scalene** and **middle scalene** muscles and lie superior and posterior to the **subclavian artery**.

### Trunks（神經幹）

The three trunks of the **brachial plexus** originate from the roots, pass laterally over rib I, and enter the axilla (Fig. 7.52):

- The **superior trunk** is formed by the union of **C5** and **C6** roots.
- The **middle trunk** is a continuation of the **C7** root.
- The **inferior trunk** is formed by the union of the **C8** and **T1** roots.

The inferior trunk lies on rib I posterior to the **subclavian artery**; the middle and superior trunks are more superior in position.

### Divisions（分支）

Each of the three trunks of the **brachial plexus** divides into an **anterior** and a **posterior division** (Fig. 7.52):

- The three anterior divisions form parts of the brachial plexus that ultimately give rise to peripheral nerves associated with the anterior compartments of the arm and forearm.
- The three posterior divisions combine to form parts of the brachial plexus that give rise to nerves associated with the posterior compartments.

> **臨床重點**：No peripheral nerves originate directly from the divisions of the brachial plexus.

### Cords（神經索）

The three cords of the **brachial plexus** originate from the divisions and are related to the second part of the **axillary artery** (Fig. 7.52):

- The **lateral cord** results from the union of the anterior divisions of the upper and middle trunks and therefore has contributions from **C5 to C7**—it is positioned lateral to the second part of the **axillary artery**.
- The **medial cord** is medial to the second part of the **axillary artery** and is the continuation of the anterior division of the inferior trunk—it contains contributions from **C8 and T1**.
- The **posterior cord** occurs posterior to the second part of the **axillary artery** and originates as the union of all three posterior divisions—it contains contributions from all roots of the brachial plexus (**C5 to T1**).

> **臨床重點**：Most of the major peripheral nerves of the upper limb originate from the cords of the brachial plexus. Generally, nerves associated with the anterior compartments of the upper limb arise from the medial and lateral cords, and nerves associated with the posterior compartments originate from the posterior cord.

---

## Branches of the Brachial Plexus（臂神經叢的分支）

### Branches of the Roots（神經根的分支）

In addition to small segmental branches from **C5 to C8** to muscles of the neck and a contribution of **C5** to the **phrenic nerve**, the roots of the **brachial plexus** give rise to the **dorsal scapular nerve** and **long thoracic nerve** (Fig. 7.53).

#### Dorsal Scapular Nerve（背肩胛神經）

The **dorsal scapular nerve**:

- originates from the **C5 root** of the brachial plexus,
- passes posteriorly, often piercing the **middle scalene muscle** in the neck, to reach and travel along the medial border of the **scapula** (Fig. 7.54), and
- innervates the **rhomboid major** and **rhomboid minor** muscles from their deep surfaces.

#### Long Thoracic Nerve（胸長神經）

The **long thoracic nerve**:

- originates from the anterior rami of **C5 to C7**,
- passes vertically down the neck, through the **axillary inlet**, and down the medial wall of the axilla to supply the **serratus anterior muscle** (Fig. 7.54), and
- lies on the superficial aspect of the **serratus anterior muscle**.

> **臨床重點**：Damage to the long thoracic nerve results in "winged scapula" due to loss of serratus anterior function.

### Branches of the Trunks（神經幹的分支）

The only branches from the trunks of the **brachial plexus** are two nerves that originate from the **superior trunk** (upper trunk): the **suprascapular nerve** and the **nerve to the subclavius muscle** (Fig. 7.53).

#### Suprascapular Nerve（肩胛上神經）

The **suprascapular nerve** (**C5** and **C6**):

- originates from the **superior trunk** of the brachial plexus,
- passes laterally through the **posterior triangle of the neck** (Fig. 7.54) and through the **suprascapular foramen** to enter the posterior scapular region,
- innervates the **supraspinatus** and **infraspinatus** muscles, and
- is accompanied in the lateral parts of the neck and in the posterior scapular region by the **suprascapular artery**.

#### Nerve to the Subclavius Muscle（鎖骨下肌神經）

The **nerve to the subclavius muscle** (**C5** and **C6**) is a small nerve that:

- originates from the **superior trunk** of the brachial plexus,
- passes anteroinferiorly over the **subclavian artery** and **vein**, and
- innervates the **subclavius muscle**.

### Branches of the Lateral Cord（外側索的分支）

Three nerves originate entirely or partly from the **lateral cord** (Fig. 7.53).

#### Lateral Pectoral Nerve（外側胸神經）

The **lateral pectoral nerve** is the most proximal of the branches from the lateral cord. It passes anteriorly, together with the **thoraco-acromial artery**, to penetrate the **clavipectoral fascia** that spans the gap between the **subclavius** and **pectoralis minor** muscles (Fig. 7.55), and innervates the **pectoralis major muscle**.

#### Musculocutaneous Nerve（肌皮神經）

The **musculocutaneous nerve** is a large terminal branch of the lateral cord. It passes laterally to penetrate the **coracobrachialis muscle** and pass between the **biceps brachii** and **brachialis** muscles in the arm, and innervates all three flexor muscles in the anterior compartment of the arm, terminating as the **lateral cutaneous nerve of the forearm**.

#### Lateral Root of the Median Nerve（正中神經外側根）

The **lateral root of the median nerve** is the largest terminal branch of the lateral cord and passes medially to join a similar branch from the medial cord to form the **median nerve** (Fig. 7.55).

### Branches of the Medial Cord（內側索的分支）

The **medial cord** has five branches (Fig. 7.55).

#### Medial Pectoral Nerve（內側胸神經）

The **medial pectoral nerve** is the most proximal branch. It receives a communicating branch from the **lateral pectoral nerve** and then passes anteriorly between the **axillary artery** and **axillary vein**. Branches of the nerve penetrate and supply the **pectoralis minor muscle**. Some of these branches pass through the muscle to reach and supply the **pectoralis major muscle**. Other branches occasionally pass around the inferior or lateral margin of the **pectoralis minor muscle** to reach the **pectoralis major muscle**.

#### Medial Cutaneous Nerve of the Arm（臂內側皮神經）

The **medial cutaneous nerve of the arm** (medial brachial cutaneous nerve) passes through the axilla and into the arm where it penetrates deep fascia and supplies skin over the medial side of the distal third of the arm. In the axilla, the nerve communicates with the **intercostobrachial nerve of T2**. Fibers of the medial cutaneous nerve of the arm innervate the upper part of the medial surface of the arm and floor of the axilla.

#### Medial Cutaneous Nerve of the Forearm（前臂內側皮神經）

The **medial cutaneous nerve of the forearm** (medial antebrachial cutaneous nerve) originates just distal to the origin of the medial cutaneous nerve of the arm. It passes out of the axilla and into the arm where it gives off a branch to the skin over the **biceps brachii muscle**, and then continues down the arm to penetrate the deep fascia with the **basilic vein**, continuing inferiorly to supply the skin over the anterior surface of the forearm. It innervates skin over the medial surface of the forearm down to the wrist.

#### Medial Root of the Median Nerve（正中神經內側根）

The **medial root of the median nerve** passes laterally to join with a similar root from the lateral cord to form the **median nerve** anterior to the third part of the **axillary artery**.

#### Ulnar Nerve（尺神經）

The **ulnar nerve** is a large terminal branch of the **medial cord** (Fig. 7.55). However, near its origin, it often receives a communicating branch from the lateral root of the median nerve originating from the lateral cord and carrying fibers from **C7**. The ulnar nerve passes through the arm and forearm into the hand where it innervates all **intrinsic muscles of the hand** (except for the three thenar muscles and the two lateral lumbrical muscles). On passing through the forearm, branches of the ulnar nerve innervate the **flexor carpi ulnaris muscle** and the **medial half of the flexor digitorum profundus muscle**. The ulnar nerve innervates skin over the palmar surface of the little finger, medial half of the ring finger, and associated palm and wrist, and the skin over the dorsal surface of the medial part of the hand.

### Median Nerve（正中神經）

The **median nerve** is formed anterior to the third part of the **axillary artery** by the union of lateral and medial roots originating from the lateral and medial cords of the **brachial plexus** (Fig. 7.55). It passes into the arm anterior to the **brachial artery** and through the arm into the forearm, where branches innervate most of the muscles in the anterior compartment of the forearm (except for the **flexor carpi ulnaris muscle** and the **medial half of the flexor digitorum profundus muscle**, which are innervated by the **ulnar nerve**).

The median nerve continues into the hand to innervate:

- the **three thenar muscles** associated with the thumb,
- the **two lateral lumbrical muscles** associated with movement of the index and middle fingers, and
- the skin over the palmar surface of the lateral three and one-half digits and over the lateral side of the palm and middle of the wrist.

> **臨床重點**：The musculocutaneous nerve, the lateral root of the median nerve, the median nerve, the medial root of the median nerve, and the ulnar nerve form an **M** over the third part of the axillary artery. This feature, together with penetration of the **coracobrachialis muscle** by the musculocutaneous nerve, can be used to identify components of the brachial plexus in the axilla.

### Branches of the Posterior Cord（後側索的分支）

Five nerves originate from the **posterior cord** of the brachial plexus:

- the **superior subscapular nerve**,
- the **thoracodorsal nerve**,
- the **inferior subscapular nerve**,
- the **axillary nerve**, and
- the **radial nerve** (Fig. 7.53).

All these nerves except the radial nerve innervate muscles associated with the posterior wall of the axilla; the radial nerve passes into the arm and forearm.

#### Superior Subsscapular Nerve（上肩胛下神經）

The **superior subscapular nerve** is short and passes into and supplies the **subscapularis muscle**.

#### Thoracodorsal Nerve（胸背神經）

The **thoracodorsal nerve** is the longest of these three nerves and passes vertically along the posterior axillary wall. It penetrates and innervates the **latissimus dorsi muscle**.

#### Inferior Subsscapular Nerve（下肩胛下神經）

The **inferior subscapular nerve** also passes inferiorly along the posterior axillary wall and innervates the **subscapularis** and **teres major** muscles.

#### Axillary Nerve（腋神經）

The **axillary nerve** originates from the posterior cord and passes inferiorly and laterally along the posterior wall to exit the axilla through the **quadrangular space** (Fig. 7.56). It passes posteriorly around the **surgical neck of the humerus** and innervates both the **deltoid** and **teres minor** muscles. A **superior lateral cutaneous nerve of the arm** originates from the axillary nerve after passing through the quadrangular space and loops around the posterior margin of the **deltoid muscle** to innervate skin in that region. The axillary nerve is accompanied by the **posterior circumflex humeral artery**.

#### Radial Nerve（橈神經）

The **radial nerve** is the largest terminal branch of the posterior cord (Fig. 7.56). It passes out of the axilla and into the posterior compartment of the arm by passing through the **triangular interval** between the inferior border of the **teres major muscle**, the **long head of the triceps brachii muscle**, and the **shaft of the humerus**. It is accompanied through the triangular interval by the **profunda brachii artery**, which originates from the **brachial artery** in the anterior compartment of the arm.

The **radial nerve** and its branches innervate:

- all muscles in the **posterior compartments of the arm and forearm**, and
- the skin on the posterior aspect of the arm and forearm, the lower lateral surface of the arm, and the dorsal lateral surface of the hand.

The **posterior cutaneous nerve of the arm** (posterior brachial cutaneous nerve) originates from the radial nerve in the axilla and innervates skin on the posterior surface of the arm.

---

## Table 7.7 — Branches of Brachial Plexus（臂神經叢分支總表）

### Branches of the Roots（神經根的分支）

| Branch | Origin | Spinal Segments | Function |
|--------|--------|-----------------|----------|
| **Dorsal scapular** | C5 root | C5 | Motor: Rhomboid major, rhomboid minor |
| **Long thoracic** | C5 to C7 roots | C5 to C7 | Motor: Serratus anterior |

### Branches of the Trunks（神經幹的分支）

| Branch | Origin | Spinal Segments | Function |
|--------|--------|-----------------|----------|
| **Suprascapular** | Superior trunk | C5, C6 | Motor: Supraspinatus, infraspinatus |
| **Nerve to subclavius** | Superior trunk | C5, C6 | Motor: Subclavius |

### Branches of the Cords（神經索的分支）

| Branch | Origin | Spinal Segments | Function |
|--------|--------|-----------------|----------|
| **Lateral pectoral** | Lateral cord | C5 to C7 | Motor: Pectoralis major |
| **Musculocutaneous** | Lateral cord | C5 to C7 | Motor: All muscles in anterior compartment of the arm; Sensory: Skin on lateral side of forearm |
| **Medial pectoral** | Medial cord | C8, T1 | Motor: Pectoralis major, pectoralis minor |
| **Medial cutaneous of arm** | Medial cord | C8, T1 | Sensory: Skin on medial side of distal one-third of arm |
| **Medial cutaneous of forearm** | Medial cord | C8, T1 | Sensory: Skin on medial side of forearm |
| **Median** | Medial and lateral cords | (C5), C6 to T1 | Motor: All muscles in anterior compartment of forearm (except flexor carpi ulnaris and medial half of flexor digitorum profundus), three thenar muscles, two lateral lumbricals; Sensory: Skin over palmar surface of lateral three and one-half digits, lateral palm, middle of wrist |
| **Ulnar** | Medial cord | (C7), C8, T1 | Motor: All intrinsic muscles of hand (except three thenar muscles and two lateral lumbricals), flexor carpi ulnaris, medial half of flexor digitorum profundus; Sensory: Skin over palmar surface of medial one and one-half digits, associated palm and wrist, dorsal surface of medial one and one-half digits |
| **Superior subscapular** | Posterior cord | C5, C6 | Motor: Subscapularis |
| **Thoracodorsal** | Posterior cord | C6 to C8 | Motor: Latissimus dorsi |
| **Inferior subscapular** | Posterior cord | C5, C6 | Motor: Subscapularis, teres major |
| **Axillary** | Posterior cord | C5, C6 | Motor: Deltoid, teres minor; Sensory: Skin over upper lateral part of arm |
| **Radial** | Posterior cord | C5 to C8, (T1) | Motor: All muscles in posterior compartments of arm and forearm; Sensory: Skin on posterior aspects of arm and forearm, lower lateral surface of arm, dorsal lateral surface of hand |

---

## In the Clinic — Clinical Considerations（臨床重點）

### Injuries to the Brachial Plexus（臂神經叢損傷）

> **臨床重點**：The brachial plexus is an extremely complex structure. When damaged, it requires meticulous clinical history taking and examination. Assessment of the individual nerve functions can be obtained by **nerve conduction studies** and **electromyography**, which assess the latency of muscle contraction when the nerve is artificially stimulated.

> **臨床重點**：Brachial plexus injuries are usually the result of **blunt trauma** producing nerve avulsions and disruption. These injuries are usually devastating for the function of the upper limb and require many months of dedicated rehabilitation for even a small amount of function to return.

> **臨床重點**：Spinal cord injuries in the cervical region and direct pulling injuries tend to affect the **roots** of the brachial plexus. Severe trauma to the first rib usually affects the **trunks**. The **divisions** and **cords** of the brachial plexus can be injured by dislocation of the **glenohumeral joint**.

---

## Axillary Lymphatics（腋淋巴系統）

All lymphatics from the **upper limb** drain into lymph nodes in the **axilla** (Fig. 7.57).

In addition, axillary nodes receive drainage from an extensive area on the adjacent trunk, which includes regions of the upper back and shoulder, the lower neck, the chest, and the upper anterolateral abdominal wall. Axillary nodes also receive drainage from approximately **75% of the mammary gland**.

### Axillary Lymph Nodes（腋淋巴結）

The **20–30 axillary nodes** are generally divided into five groups on the basis of location:

- **Humeral (lateral) nodes**: Posteromedial to the **axillary vein**, receive most of the lymphatic drainage from the upper limb.
- **Pectoral (anterior) nodes**: Along the inferior margin of the **pectoralis minor muscle** along the course of the **lateral thoracic vessels**, receive drainage from the abdominal wall, the chest, and the **mammary gland**.
- **Subscapular (posterior) nodes**: On the posterior axillary wall in association with the **subscapular vessels**, drain the posterior axillary wall and receive lymphatics from the back, the shoulder, and the neck.
- **Central nodes**: Embedded in axillary fat, receive tributaries from humeral, subscapular, and pectoral groups of nodes.
- **Apical nodes**: The most superior group of nodes in the axilla, drain all other groups of nodes in the region. In addition, they receive lymphatic vessels that accompany the **cephalic vein** as well as vessels that drain the superior region of the **mammary gland**.

> **臨床重點**：Efferent vessels from the apical group converge to form the **subclavian trunk**, which usually joins the venous system at the junction between the right subclavian vein and the right internal jugular vein in the neck. On the left, the subclavian trunk usually joins the **thoracic duct** in the base of the neck.

### Breast Cancer（乳癌）

> **臨床重點**：Lymphatic drainage from the lateral part of the breast passes through nodes in the axilla. Significant disruption to the normal lymphatic drainage of the upper limb may occur if a **mastectomy** or a **surgical axillary nodal clearance** has been carried out for breast cancer. Furthermore, some patients have **radiotherapy** to the axilla to prevent the spread of metastatic disease, but a side effect of this is the destruction of the tiny lymphatics as well as the cancer cells.

> **臨床重點**：If the lymphatic drainage of the upper limb is damaged, the arm may swell and **pitting edema (lymphedema)** may develop.

### Axillary Process of the Mammary Gland（乳腺腋突）

Although the **mammary gland** is in superficial fascia overlying the thoracic wall, its superolateral region extends along the inferior margin of the **pectoralis major muscle** toward the axilla. In some cases, this may pass around the margin of the muscle to penetrate deep fascia and enter the axilla (Fig. 7.58). This **axillary process** rarely reaches as high as the apex of the axilla.

# Gray's Anatomy — Head & Neck

---

## Upper Limb — ARM（臂部）

### 概述

**臂部（Arm）** 是介於**肩部（shoulder）**與**肘部（elbow）**之間的上肢區域（圖 7.59）。臂部上方經內側與**腋窩（axilla）**相通。下方的**肘窩（cubital fossa）**是許多重要結構由臂部通往前臂的通道，位於**肘關節（elbow joint）**前方。

臂部由**內側肌間隔（medial intermuscular septum）**與**外側肌間隔（lateral intermuscular septum）**分隔為兩個腔室，這些肌間隔從**肱骨（humerus）**兩側延伸至包覆肢體的深筋膜（圖 7.59）。

- **前腔室（Anterior compartment）**：主要含**屈肌**，負責**屈肘關節**
- **後腔室（Posterior compartment）**：含**伸肌**，負責**伸肘關節**

主要神經與血管供應並穿行於各腔室。

> **臨床重點**：臂部兩個腔室的分隔結構具有臨床意義，例如前臂腔室症候群（compartment syndrome）可能發生於單一腔室，需精確區分受影響部位。

---

### 骨骼（Bones）

臂部的骨骼支撐為**肱骨（humerus）**（圖 7.60）。臂部多數大型肌肉均附著於前臂兩骨——**橈骨（radius）**與**尺骨（ulna）**的近端，負責在肘關節處屈伸前臂。此外，主要分布於前臂、支配手部運動的肌肉，則起源於肱骨遠端。

#### 肱骨幹與遠端

從橫切面看，肱骨幹呈三角形，具有：

- **前緣（anterior border）**、**外側緣（lateral border）**、**內側緣（medial border）**
- **前外側面（anterolateral surface）**、**前內側面（anteromedial surface）**、**後面（posterior surface）**（圖 7.60）

**後面**上方有一線性粗糙區域，為**肱三頭肌外側頭（lateral head of triceps brachii）**的附著處，該附著處始於**外科頸（surgical neck）**下方，斜向延伸至**三角肌粗隆（deltoid tuberosity）**。

> **臨床重點**：肱骨外科頸骨折常伴隨**腋神經（axillary nerve）**損傷，因神經繞過外科頸後方行走於肩關節周圍。

後面中部及前外側面相鄰區域有淺**橈神經溝（radial groove）**，斜向通過骨頭，與三角肌粗隆後緣平行。**橈神經（radial nerve）**與**肱深動脈（profunda brachii artery）**行走於此溝中。

約在骨幹中段，內側緣有一細長粗糙區域，為**喙肱肌（coracobrachialis）**附著處。

分隔前、後腔室的內、外側肌間隔，分別附著於內側緣與外側緣（圖 7.61）。

**遠端**：骨頭變扁平，這些骨緣擴展為**外側髁上嵴（lateral supraepicondylar ridge）**與**內側髁上嵴（medial supraepicondylar ridge）**。外側髁上嵴較內側嵴更明顯，粗糙區域為前臂後腔室肌肉的附著處。

肱骨遠端呈扁平狀（前後向），具有**髁（condyle）**、兩個**髁上突（epicondyle）**及三個**窩（fossa）**（圖 7.61）。

##### 髁（Condyle）

髁的兩個關節部分——**肱骨小頭（capitulum）**與**滑車（trochlea）**——與前臂兩骨形成關節：

- **Capitulum（肱骨小頭）**：與前臂**橈骨**關節。位於外側，呈半球形，向前並略向下突出。從肱骨後方觀察時不可見。
- **Trochlea（滑車）**：與前臂**尺骨**關節。 Pulley 形狀，位於 capitulum 內側。內側緣較外側緣更突出，與 capitulum 不同的是，它延伸至骨頭後面。

##### 兩個髁上突（Epicondyles）

兩個髁上突緊鄰並略上位於 trochlea 與 capitulum 上方（圖 7.61）。

- **內側髁上突（Medial epicondyle）**：大型骨性突起，是肘內側主要可觸及的標誌，從肱骨遠端向內側突出。表面有大橢圓形壓跡，為前臂前腔室肌肉的附著處。**尺神經（ulnar nerve）**從臂部進入前臂，繞過內側髁上突後方，可在此處觸及其緊贴骨頭的走向。

> **臨床重點**：尺神經在內側髁上突後方的通道是臨床上重要的解剖位置。尺神經炎（ulnar neuritis）或肘部尺神經卡壓（cubital tunnel syndrome）常在此區域發生，表現為手部感覺異常及精細動作障礙。

- **外側髁上突（ Lateral epicondyle）**：比內側髁上突小很多。位於 capitulum 外側，有不規則大壓跡，為前臂後腔室肌肉的附著處。

##### 三個窩（Fossae）

三個窩位於 trochlea 與 capitulum 上方的肱骨遠端（圖 7.61）。

- **橈骨窩（Radial fossa）**：三窩中最不明顯，位於肱骨前面、capitulum 正上方。
- **冠狀窩（Coronoid fossa）**：緊鄰 radial fossa，位於 trochlea 上方。
- **鷹嘴窩（Olecranon fossa）**：三窩中最大，位於肱骨遠端後面、trochlea 正上方。

三窩在肘關節運動時容納前臂骨的突起。

#### 橈骨近端（Proximal end of the radius）

橈骨近端由**頭（head）**、**頸（neck）**與**橈骨粗隆（radial tuberosity）**組成（圖 7.62A,B）。

- **橈骨頭（Head of radius）**：厚盤狀結構，位於水平面。圓形上面凹陷，與肱骨 capitulum 形成關節。厚邊緣在內側寬闊，與尺骨近端**橈骨切跡（radial notch）**形成關節。
- **橈骨頸（ Neck of radius）**：頭與骨幹上 radial tuberosity 之間的短窄圓柱狀骨。
- **橈骨粗隆（Radial tuberosity）**：橈骨內側面的高大鈍性突起，緊鄰頸下方。大部分表面粗糙，為**肱二頭肌腱（biceps brachii tendon）**附著處。**橈骨斜線（oblique line of radius）**從 radial tuberosity 下緣斜向延伸穿過骨幹。

#### 尺骨近端（Proximal end of the ulna）

尺骨近端遠比橈骨近端大，由**鷹嘴（olecranon）**、**冠狀突（coronoid process）**、**滑車切跡（trochlear notch）**、**橈骨切跡（radial notch）**與**尺骨粗隆（tuberosity of the ulna）**組成（圖 7.63A,B）。

- **Olecranon（鷹嘴）**：大型骨性突起，從尺骨向上延伸。前外側面為關節面，參與構成與肱骨滑車關節的 trochlear notch。上面有大粗糙壓跡，為**肱三頭肌（triceps brachii）**附著處。後面光滑，略呈三角形，可觸及為「肘尖」。
- **冠狀突（Coronoid process）**：從尺骨近端向前突出（圖 7.63）。其上前外側面為關節面，與 olecranon 共同構成滑車切跡。外側面有**橈骨切跡**與橈骨頭形成關節。
- 緊鄰 radial notch 下方有一窩，允許 radial tuberosity 在旋前與旋後時改變位置。此窩後緣擴展形成**旋後肌嵴（supinator crest）**。冠狀突前面呈三角形，尖端指向遠端，有多個肌肉附著粗糙區。最大粗糙區位於前面尖端的**尺骨粗隆**，是**肱肌（brachialis）**的附著點。

---

### 肌肉（Muscles）

#### 前腔室（Anterior Compartment）

臂部前腔室含三塊肌肉——**Coracobrachialis（喙肱肌）**、**Brachialis（肱肌）**與**Biceps brachii（肱二頭肌）**，主要由**肌皮神經（musculocutaneous nerve）**支配。

##### Coracobrachialis（喙肱肌）

**Coracobrachialis** 從肩胛骨**喙突（coracoid process）**尖端延伸至肱骨幹中段內側（圖 7.64 及表 7.8）。它穿過腋窩，被肌皮神經穿過並支配。

功能：**屈臂（Flexes the arm）**。

##### Biceps brachii（肱二頭肌）

**Biceps brachii** 有兩個頭：

- **短頭（Short head）**：與 coracobrachialis 共同起源於 coracoid process。
- **長頭（Long head）**：以肌腱起源於肩胛骨的**盂上結節（supraglenoid tubercle）**（圖 7.64 及表 7.8）。

長頭肌腱穿過**盂肱關節（glenohumeral joint）**，位於肱骨頭上方，隨後穿過**結節間溝（intertubercular sulcus）**進入臂部。在臂部，肌腱與肌肉會合，與短頭的肌肉一起覆蓋於 brachialis 上。

兩頭會合形成單一肌腱，附著於**橈骨粗隆（radial tuberosity）**。當肌腱進入前臂時，從內側展開一片扁平結締組織（**二頭肌腱膜（bicipital aponeurosis）**），融入覆蓋前臂前腔室的深筋膜。

功能：
- 強力的**前臂屈肌**（在肘關節處）
- 當肘關節屈曲時，**最有力的前臂旋後肌**
- 因為兩個頭都跨越盂肱關節，亦可**屈肩關節**

> **臨床重點**：臨床上，叩擊肘部的肱二頭肌腱用於測試主要是**脊髓第6節段（C6）**的功能完整性。

**Brachialis（肱肌）**：起源於肱骨前面遠端一半及相鄰的肌間隔（特別是內側）（圖 7.64 及表 7.8）。位於 biceps brachii 深面，腹背扁平，匯聚成肌腱附著於尺骨粗隆。

功能：**屈前臂（在肘關節處）**。

支配：主要由**肌皮神經**支配。外側小部分由**橈神經**支配。

> **臨床重點**：二頭肌腱rupture（斷裂）
> 
> 上肢肌肉與肌腱的斷裂並不常見，但最常斷裂的肌腱是**肱二頭肌長頭腱**。單獨發生時對上肢功能影響不大，但會產生特徵性畸形——屈肘時肌肉腹部異常突出，稱為「**Popeye 徵象**」。

##### 總表：前臂腔室肌肉（表 7.8）

| 肌肉 | 起源 | 附著 | 神經支配 | 功能 |
|------|------|------|----------|------|
| **Coracobrachialis** | Coracoid process 尖端 | 肱骨幹中段線性粗糙區 | Musculocutaneous nerve (C5, C6, C7) | 屈肩關節 |
| **Biceps brachii** | 長頭：Scapula 盂上結節；短頭：Coracoid process 尖端 | Radial tuberosity | Musculocutaneous nerve (C5, C6) | 強力的前臂屈肌與旋後肌；輔助屈肩關節 |
| **Brachialis** | 肱骨前面遠端一半及相鄰肌間隔 | 尺骨粗隆 | Musculocutaneous nerve (C5, C6)；橈神經（C7）支配外側小部分 | 強力的前臂屈肌 |

---

#### 後腔室（Posterior Compartment）

臂部後腔室唯一的肌肉是**Triceps brachii（肱三頭肌）**（圖 7.65 及表 7.9），由**橈神經（radial nerve）**支配。

**Triceps brachii** 有三個頭：

- **長頭（Long head）**：起源於肩胛骨**盂下結節（infraglenoid tubercle）**
- **內側頭（Medial head）**：起源於肱骨幹後面、橈神經溝下方的大範圍區域
- **外側頭（Lateral head）**：起源於肱骨橈神經溝上方線性粗糙區

三頭會合形成粗大肌腱，附著於尺骨**olecranon** 上方。

功能：**伸前臂（在肘關節處）**。長頭亦可伸並內收肩關節。

> **臨床重點**：臨床上，叩擊肱三頭肌腱用於測試主要是**脊髓第7節段（C7）**的功能完整性。

##### 總表：後臂腔室肌肉（表 7.9）

| 肌肉 | 起源 | 附著 | 神經支配 | 功能 |
|------|------|------|----------|------|
| **Triceps brachii** | 長頭：Scapula 盂下結節；內側頭：肱骨後面；外側頭：肱骨後面 | Olecranon | Radial nerve (C6, C7, C8) | 伸前臂（在肘關節處）；長頭亦可伸並內收肩關節 |

---

### 動靜脈（Arteries and Veins）

#### 臂动脉（Brachial Artery）

臂部主要动脉——**Brachial artery（臂动脉）**位於前腔室（圖 7.66A）。在**大圓肌（teres major）**下緣延續**腋动脉（axillary artery）**，於肘關節遠端分為**橈动脉（radial artery）**與**尺动脉（ulnar artery）**。

近端臂部，臂动脉位於內側。遠端臂部，它移向外側，位於肱骨**外側髁上突**與**內側髁上突**之間的中點。它越過肘關節前方，位於**肱二頭肌腱**的內側。臂动脉全程可觸及。近端可將其壓向肱骨內側以止血。

臂动脉在臂部的分支包括：

- 鄰近肌肉的分支
- 兩個**尺側副血管（ulnar collateral vessels）**，參與肘關節周圍动脉網絡（圖 7.66B）
- **肱深动脉（Profunda brachii artery）**——營養肱骨的血管，穿過骨幹前內側面孔

> **臨床重點**：臂动脉是上肢主要的搏動性止血點。在創傷或手術中，可於臂部中段將臂动脉壓向肱骨以控制出血。

##### 肱深动脉（Profunda Brachii Artery）

**Profunda brachii artery** 是臂动脉最大的分支，進入並供應臂部後腔室（圖 7.66A,B）。它與橈神經一起進入後腔室，兩者穿過**三角間隙（triangular interval）**——由肱骨幹、大圓肌下緣與長頭外側緣構成。隨後沿肱骨後面**橈神經溝**行走，位於肱三頭肌外側頭深面。

分支供應鄰近肌肉，並與**旋肱後动脉（posterior circumflex humeral artery）**吻合。最終分為兩條側支，參與肘關節周圍动脉吻合網絡（圖 7.66B）。

> **臨床重點**：血壓測量
> 
> 血壓是極重要的生理參數。高血壓（高血壓病）需治療以預防長期併發症如中風。低血壓可由嚴重失血、廣泛感染或心輸出不良（如心肌梗塞後）引起。
> 
> 多數臨床醫師使用**血壓計（sphygmomanometer）**與**聽診器**測量血壓。血壓計的壓脈帶纏繞臂部中段，壓迫臂动脉向肱骨。壓脈帶充氣使壓力超過收縮壓（大於 120 mm Hg）。醫師將聽診器置於肘窩的臂动脉上聆聽脈搏。當血壓計壓力降至收縮壓以下時，脈搏變為可聽到的規律敲擊聲。當壓力繼續下降至舒張壓以下時，敲擊聲消失。正常範圍為 120/80 mm Hg（收縮壓/舒張壓）。

#### 靜脈（Veins）

成對的**臂靜脈（brachial veins）**沿臂动脉內外側行走，接受伴隨动脉分支的靜脈屬支（圖 7.67）。

此外，臂部還有兩條大型皮下靜脈——**Basilic vein（貴要靜脈）**與**Cephalic vein（頭靜脈）**。

- **Basilic vein（貴要靜脈）**：垂直行走於臂部遠端一半，穿過深筋膜位於臂动脉內側，於大圓肌下緣成為**腋靜脈（axillary vein）**。臂靜脈匯入貴要靜脈或腋靜脈。
- **Cephalic vein（頭靜脈）**：沿臂部前外側向上行走，穿過腋窩前壁匯入腋靜脈。

---

> **臨床重點摘要**
> 
> - **內側髁上突**是臨床上可觸及的重要解剖標誌，**尺神經**繞過其後方行走
> - **橈神經**在**橈神經溝**行走，骨折或長期壓迫可造成損傷
> - **臂动脉**在臂部中段可觸及並壓迫止血
> - 血壓測量使用**血壓計**與**聽診器**，正常值 120/80 mm Hg
> - **二頭肌長頭腱斷裂**產生特徵性「Popeye 徵象」

---

# Gray's Anatomy — Upper Limb: Arm, Elbow & Forearm

---

## 上臂的神經（Nerves of the Arm）

### Musculocutaneous Nerve

The musculocutaneous nerve leaves the axilla and enters the arm by passing through the **coracobrachialis muscle** (Fig. 7.68). It passes diagonally down the arm in the plane between the **biceps brachii** and **brachialis** muscles. After giving rise to motor branches in the arm, it emerges laterally to the tendon of the biceps brachii muscle at the elbow, penetrates deep fascia, and continues as the **lateral cutaneous nerve of forearm**.

The musculocutaneous nerve provides:
- Motor innervation to all muscles in the **anterior compartment of the arm**
- Sensory innervation to skin on the **lateral surface of the forearm**

> **臨床重點**：Musculocutaneous nerve injury is uncommon due to its relatively protected position. When injured, patients lose elbow flexion (biceps brachii, brachialis) and have numbness over the lateral forearm.

---

### Median Nerve

The median nerve enters the arm from the axilla at the inferior margin of the **teres major muscle** (Fig. 7.68). It passes vertically down the medial side of the arm in the anterior compartment and is related to the **brachial artery** throughout its course:

- In proximal regions, the median nerve is immediately **lateral** to the brachial artery.
- In more distal regions, the median nerve crosses to the **medial side** of the brachial artery and lies anterior to the elbow joint.

The median nerve has no major branches in the arm, but a branch to the **pronator teres muscle** may originate immediately proximal to the elbow joint.

---

### Ulnar Nerve

The ulnar nerve enters the arm with the median nerve and **axillary artery** (Fig. 7.68). It passes through proximal regions medial to the axillary artery. In the middle of the arm, the ulnar nerve penetrates the **medial intermuscular septum** and enters the posterior compartment where it lies anterior to the **medial head of the triceps brachii** muscle. It passes posterior to the **medial epicondyle of the humerus** and then into the anterior compartment of the forearm.

The ulnar nerve has **no major branches in the arm**.

---

### Radial Nerve

The radial nerve originates from the **posterior cord of the brachial plexus** and enters the arm by crossing the inferior margin of the **teres major muscle** (Fig. 7.69). As it enters the arm, it lies posterior to the **brachial artery**. Accompanied by the **profunda brachii artery**, the radial nerve enters the posterior compartment of the arm by passing through the **triangular interval**.

As the radial nerve passes diagonally, from medial to lateral, through the posterior compartment, it lies in the **radial groove** directly on bone. On the lateral side of the arm, it passes anteriorly through the **lateral intermuscular septum** and enters the anterior compartment where it lies between the **brachialis** muscle and the **brachioradialis** muscle, which attaches to the **lateral supraepicondylar ridge of the humerus**. The radial nerve enters the forearm anterior to the **lateral epicondyle of the humerus**, just deep to the brachioradialis muscle.

In the arm, the radial nerve has muscular and cutaneous branches (Fig. 7.69):

- **Muscular branches** include those to the **triceps brachii**, **brachioradialis**, and **extensor carpi radialis longus** muscles. In addition, the radial nerve contributes to the innervation of the lateral part of the **brachialis** muscle. One of the branches to the **medial head of the triceps brachii** muscle arises before the radial nerve's entrance into the posterior compartment and passes vertically down the arm in association with the ulnar nerve.
- **Cutaneous branches** of the radial nerve that originate in the posterior compartment of the arm are the **inferior lateral cutaneous nerve of the arm** and the **posterior cutaneous nerve of the forearm**, both of which penetrate through the lateral head of the triceps brachii muscle and the overlying deep fascia to become subcutaneous.

> **臨床重點**：Radial nerve injury in the arm — The radial nerve is tightly bound with the profunda brachii artery between the medial and lateral heads of the triceps brachii muscle in the radial groove. If the humerus is fractured, the radial nerve may become stretched or transected in this region, leading to permanent damage and loss of function. This injury is typical and the nerve should always be tested when a fracture of the midshaft of the humerus is suspected. The patient's symptoms usually include **wrist drop** (due to denervation of the extensor muscles) and sensory changes over the dorsum of the hand.

> **臨床重點**：Median nerve injury in the arm — In the arm and forearm the median nerve is usually not injured by trauma because of its relatively deep position. The commonest neurological problem associated with the median nerve is compression beneath the flexor retinaculum at the wrist (carpal tunnel syndrome). On very rare occasions, a fibrous band may arise from the anterior aspect of the humerus beneath which the median nerve passes. This is an embryological remnant of the coracobrachialis muscle and is sometimes called the **ligament of Struthers**; occasionally, it may calcify. This band can compress the median nerve, resulting in weakness of the flexor muscles in the forearm and the thenar muscles. Nerve conduction studies will demonstrate the site of nerve compression.

---

## 肘關節（Elbow Joint）

The elbow joint is a **complex joint** involving three separate articulations, which share a common **synovial cavity** (Fig. 7.71):

- The joints between the **trochlear notch of the ulna** and the **trochlea of the humerus** and between the **head of the radius** and the **capitulum of the humerus** are primarily involved with hinge-like flexion and extension of the forearm on the arm and, together, are the principal articulations of the elbow joint.
- The joint between the **head of the radius** and the **radial notch of the ulna**, the **proximal radio-ulnar joint**, is involved with pronation and supination of the forearm.

The articular surfaces of the bones are covered with **hyaline cartilage**.

### Synovial Membrane

The synovial membrane originates from the edges of the articular cartilage and lines the **radial fossa**, the **coronoid fossa**, the **olecranon fossa**, the deep surface of the joint capsule, and the medial surface of the **trochlea** (Fig. 7.72).

The synovial membrane is separated from the fibrous membrane of the joint capsule by pads of **fat** in regions overlying the coronoid fossa, the olecranon fossa, and the radial fossa. These fat pads accommodate the related bony processes during extension and flexion of the elbow. Attachments of the **brachialis** and **triceps brachii** muscles to the joint capsule overlying these regions pull the attached fat pads out of the way when the adjacent bony processes are moved into the fossae.

### Joint Capsule and Ligaments

The **fibrous membrane** of the joint capsule overlies the synovial membrane, encloses the joint, and attaches to the **medial epicondyle** and the margins of the olecranon, coronoid, and radial fossae of the humerus (Fig. 7.73). It also attaches to the **coronoid process** and **olecranon of the ulna**. On the lateral side, the free inferior margin of the joint capsule passes around the **neck of the radius** from an anterior attachment to the coronoid process of the ulna to a posterior attachment to the base of the olecranon.

The fibrous membrane of the joint capsule is thickened medially and laterally to form **collateral ligaments**, which support the flexion and extension movements of the elbow joint (Fig. 7.73):

- **Ulnar collateral ligament** (medial)
- **Radial collateral ligament** (lateral)

In addition, the external surface of the joint capsule is reinforced laterally where it cuffs the head of the radius with a strong **anular ligament of the radius**. Although this ligament blends with the fibrous membrane of the joint capsule in most regions, they are separate posteriorly. The anular ligament of the radius also blends with the **radial collateral ligament**.

The **anular ligament of the radius** and related joint capsule allow the **radial head** to slide against the **radial notch of the ulna** and pivot on the capitulum during pronation and supination.

The deep surface of the fibrous membrane of the joint capsule and the related **anular ligament of the radius** that articulate with the sides of the radial head are lined by cartilage. A pocket of synovial membrane (**sacciform recess**) protrudes from the inferior free margin of the joint capsule and facilitates rotation of the radial head during pronation and supination.

### Vascular Supply and Innervation

- **Vascular supply**: through an anastomotic network of vessels derived from collateral and recurrent branches of the **brachial**, **profunda brachii**, **radial**, and **ulnar arteries**.
- **Innervation**: predominantly by branches of the **radial** and **musculocutaneous nerves**, but there may be some innervation by branches of the **ulnar** and **median nerves**.

> **臨床重點**：Supracondylar fracture of the humerus — Elbow injuries in children may result in a transverse fracture of the distal end of the humerus, above the level of the epicondyles. This fracture is termed a **supracondylar fracture**. The distal fragment and its soft tissues are pulled posteriorly by the triceps muscle. This posterior displacement effectively "bowstrings" the **brachial artery** over the irregular proximal fracture fragment. In children, this is a relatively devastating injury: the muscles of the anterior compartment of the forearm are rendered ischemic and form severe contractions, significantly reducing the function of the anterior compartment and flexor muscles (**Volkmann's ischemic contracture**).

> **臨床重點**：Pulled elbow — Pulled elbow is a disorder that typically occurs in children under 5 years of age. It is commonly caused by a sharp pull of the child's hand, usually when the child is pulled up a curb. The not-yet-developed head of the radius and the laxity of the **anular ligament of the radius** allow the head to sublux from this cuff of tissue. Pulled elbow is extremely painful, but can be treated easily by simple supination and compression of the elbow joint by the clinician. When the radial head is relocated the pain subsides immediately and the child can continue with normal activity.

> **臨床重點**：Developmental changes in the elbow joint — The elbow joint can be injured in many ways; the types of injuries are age dependent. When a fracture or soft tissue trauma is suspected, a plain lateral and an anteroposterior radiograph are obtained. In an adult it is usually not difficult to interpret the radiograph, but in children additional factors require interpretation. As the elbow develops in children, numerous secondary ossification centers appear before and around puberty. It is easy to mistakenly interpret these as fractures. In addition, it is also possible for the epiphyses and apophyses to be "pulled off" or disrupted. Therefore, when interpreting a child's radiograph of the elbow, the physician must know the child's age. Fusion occurs at around the time of puberty. An understanding of the normal epiphyses and apophyses and their normal relationship to the bones will secure a correct diagnosis. The approximate ages of appearance of the secondary ossification centers around the elbow joint are:
> - **Capitulum** — 1 year
> - **Head of radius** — 5 years
> - **Medial epicondyle** — 5 years
> - **Trochlea** — 11 years
> - **Olecranon** — 12 years
> - **Lateral epicondyle** — 13 years

> **臨床重點**：Fracture of the head of the radius — A fracture of the head of the radius is a common injury and can cause appreciable morbidity. It is one of the typical injuries that occur with a fall on the outstretched hand. On falling, the force is transmitted to the radial head, which fractures. These fractures typically result in loss of full extension, and potential surgical reconstruction may require long periods of physiotherapy to obtain a full range of movement at the elbow joint. A lateral radiograph of a fracture of the head of the radius typically demonstrates the secondary phenomenon of this injury. When the bone is fractured, fluid fills the synovial cavity, elevating the small pad of fat within the coronoid and olecranon fossae. These fat pads appear as areas of lucency on the lateral radiograph — the **"fat pad" sign**. This radiological finding is useful because fracture of the head of the radius is not always clearly visible. If there is an appropriate clinical history, tenderness around the head of the radius, and a positive fat pad sign, a fracture can be inferred clinically even if no fracture can be identified on the radiograph.

> **臨床重點**："Tennis" and "golfer's" elbow (epicondylitis) — It is not uncommon for people who are involved in sports such as golf and tennis to develop an overuse strain of the origins of the flexor and extensor muscles of the forearm. The pain is typically around the epicondyles and usually resolves after rest. If pain and inflammation persist, surgical division of the extensor or flexor origin from the bone may be necessary. Typically, in tennis players this pain occurs on the **lateral epicondyle** and **common extensor origin** (tennis elbow), whereas in golfers it occurs on the **medial epicondyle** and **common flexor origin** (golfer's elbow).

> **臨床重點**：Elbow arthritis — Osteoarthritis is extremely common and is usually most severe in the dominant limb. From time to time an arthritic elbow may undergo such degenerative change that small bone fragments appear in the articular cavity. Given the relatively small joint space, these fragments can result in an appreciable reduction in flexion and extension, and typically lodge within the **olecranon** and **coronoid fossae**.

> **臨床重點**：Ulnar nerve injury at the elbow — Posterior to the **medial epicondyle of the humerus** the ulnar nerve is bound in a fibro-osseous tunnel (the **cubital tunnel**) by a retinaculum. Older patients may develop degenerative changes within this tunnel, which compresses the ulnar nerve when flexed. The repeated action of flexion and extension of the elbow may cause local nerve damage, resulting in impaired function of the ulnar nerve. Accessory muscles and localized neuritis in this region secondary to direct trauma may also produce ulnar nerve damage.

---

## 肘窩（Cubital Fossa）

The cubital fossa is an important area of transition between the arm and the forearm. It is located **anterior to the elbow joint** and is a triangular depression formed between two forearm muscles:

- The **brachioradialis** muscle originating from the **lateral supra-epicondylar ridge of the humerus**
- The **pronator teres** muscle originating from the **medial epicondyle of the humerus** (Fig. 7.75A)

The **base** of the triangle is an imaginary horizontal line between the medial and lateral epicondyles. The **bed** or **floor** of the fossa is formed mainly by the **brachialis muscle**.

### Major Contents of the Cubital Fossa

From **lateral to medial**, the major contents are:

1. **Tendon of the biceps brachii muscle**
2. **Brachial artery**
3. **Median nerve** (Fig. 7.75B)

The **brachial artery** normally bifurcates into the **radial** and **ulnar arteries** in the apex of the fossa (Fig. 7.75B), although this bifurcation may occur much higher in the arm, even in the axilla. When taking a blood pressure reading from a patient, the clinician places the stethoscope over the brachial artery in the cubital fossa.

The **median nerve** lies immediately medial to the brachial artery and leaves the fossa by passing between the **ulnar** and **humeral heads of the pronator teres muscle** (Fig. 7.75C).

The **brachial artery** and the **median nerve** are covered and protected anteriorly in the distal part of the cubital fossa by the **bicipital aponeurosis** (Fig. 7.75B). This flat connective tissue membrane passes between the medial side of the tendon of the biceps brachii muscle and deep fascia of the forearm.

### Radial Nerve in the Cubital Fossa

The **radial nerve** lies just under the lip of the **brachioradialis muscle**, which forms the lateral margin of the fossa (Fig. 7.75C). In this position, the radial nerve divides into:

- **Superficial branch**: continues into the forearm just deep to the brachioradialis muscle
- **Deep branch**: passes between the two heads of the **supinator muscle** to access the posterior compartment of the forearm

> **臨床重點**：Construction of a dialysis fistula — Many patients throughout the world require renal dialysis for kidney failure. The patient's blood is filtered and cleaned by the dialysis machine. Blood therefore has to be taken from patients into the filtering device and then returned to them. This process of dialysis occurs over many hours and requires considerable flow rates of 250–500 ml per minute. To enable such large volumes of blood to be removed from and returned to the body, the blood is taken from vessels that have a high flow. As no veins in the peripheral limbs have such high flow, a surgical procedure is necessary to create such a system. In most patients, the **radial artery** is anastomosed (joined) to the **cephalic vein** at the wrist, or the **brachial artery** is anastomosed to the cephalic vein at the elbow. After six weeks, the veins increase in size in response to their arterial blood flow and are amenable to direct cannulation for dialysis.

### Roof of the Cubital Fossa

The **roof** of the cubital fossa is formed by **superficial fascia** and **skin**. The most important structure within the roof is the **median cubital vein** (Fig. 7.75D), which passes diagonally across the roof and connects the **cephalic vein** on the lateral side of the upper limb with the **basilic vein** on the medial side. The **bicipital aponeurosis** separates the median cubital vein from the brachial artery and median nerve.

Other structures within the roof are **cutaneous nerves** — the **medial cutaneous** and **lateral cutaneous nerves of the forearm**.

The **ulnar nerve** does not pass through the cubital fossa. Instead, it passes **posterior to the medial epicondyle**.

---

## 前臂（Forearm）

The forearm is the part of the upper limb that extends between the **elbow joint** and the **wrist joint**. Proximally, most major structures pass between the arm and forearm through, or in relation to, the cubital fossa, which is anterior to the elbow joint (Fig. 7.77). The exception is the **ulnar nerve**, which passes posterior to the **medial epicondyle of the humerus**.

Distally, structures pass between the forearm and the hand through, or anterior to, the **carpal tunnel** (Fig. 7.77). The major exception is the **radial artery**, which passes dorsally around the wrist to enter the hand posteriorly.

### Compartments of the Forearm

The forearm is divided into:

- **Anterior compartment** — contains flexor muscles, median and ulnar nerves, and brachial artery
- **Posterior compartment** — contains extensor muscles, radial nerve, and posterior interosseous vessels

The two compartments are separated by the **interosseous membrane**, the **lateral intermuscular septum**, and the **deep fascia**.

> **臨床重點**：Compartment syndromes — Fractures of the forearm bones or supracondylar fractures can lead to increased pressure within the anterior or posterior compartments. This can compromise blood flow and nerve function, leading to Volkmann's ischemic contracture if not treated promptly. Clinical signs include pain with passive stretch, swelling, and eventual neurological deficits.

# Head & Neck — Gray's Anatomy Segment 8
## Forearm Bones & Joints

前臂的骨骼框架由兩塊平行骨組成：**radius（橈骨）** 和 **ulna（尺骨）**。Radius 位於外側，近端較細，與肱骨形成關節；遠端較寬，與腕骨形成腕關節。Ulna 位於內側，其近端和遠端的尺寸與 radius 相反：近端粗大，遠端細小。Radius 和 ulna 之間的近端和遠端關節允許 radius 遠端繞著 ulna 滑動，產生手掌的**pronation（旋前）**和 **supination（旋後）**。

與手臂一樣，前臂分為**前側 compartment（肌室）**和**後側 compartment（肌室）**。這些 compartments 由以下結構分開：

- **lateral intermuscular septum（外側肌間隔）**：從 radius 前緣延伸至肢體深層筋膜
- **interosseous membrane（骨間膜）**：連接 radius 和 ulna 沿大部分長度的相鄰邊界
- 深層筋膜沿 ulna 後緣的附著

---

## Radius（橈骨）— 骨幹與遠端

Radius 的骨幹近端狹窄，與 radial tuberosity 和頸部連續；遠端則明顯寬闊。

在大部分長度中，radius 骨幹呈三角形橫切面，具有：

- **三條邊界（borders）**：anterior border、posterior border、interosseous border
- **三個面（surfaces）**：anterior surface、posterior surface、lateral surface

**Anterior border** 起於骨骼內側的 radial tuberosity 延續。在骨骼的上三分之一處，它作為 radius 的 **oblique line（斜線）** 從內側斜向穿過骨幹至外側。**Posterior border** 僅在骨幹中三分之一處明顯。**Interosseous border** 銳利，是 **interosseous membrane（骨間膜）** 的附著處，連接 radius 和 ulna。

Radius 的 **anterior surface** 和 **posterior surface** 通常光滑，而 lateral surface 大約中段有一個橢圓形粗糙區，用於 **pronator teres** 的附著。

從前方看，radius 的**遠端**寬闊且前後扁平。因此，radius 具有寬大的 anterior 和 posterior surfaces，以及狹窄的 medial 和 lateral surfaces。**Posterior surface** 的特徵是存在一個大型 **dorsal tubercle（背側結節）**，作為拇指伸肌之一（**extensor pollicis longus**）的滑輪。**Medial surface** 有與 ulna 遠端關節的明顯關節面。**Lateral surface** 呈菱形，向遠端延伸為 **radial styloid process（橈骨莖突）**。

骨骼遠端有兩個關節面，與兩塊腕骨（**scaphoid** 和 **lunate**）關節。

---

## Ulna（尺骨）— 骨幹與遠端

Ulna 的骨幹上部寬闊，與較大的近端連續；下部狹窄，形成較小的遠端頭部。與 radius 一樣，ulna 骨幹呈三角形橫切面，具有：

- **三條邊界（borders）**：anterior border、posterior border、interosseous border
- **三個面（surfaces）**：anterior surface、posterior surface、medial surface

**Anterior border** 光滑且圓鈍。**Posterior border** 銳利且沿整個長度可觸及。**Interosseous border** 同樣銳利，是 **interosseous membrane** 的附著處，連接 ulna 和 radius。

Ulna 的 **anterior surface** 光滑，但在遠端有明顯的線性粗糙區，用於 **pronator quadratus** 肌肉的附著。**Medial surface** 光滑且無明顯特徵。**Posterior surface** 有線條，分隔不同的肌肉附著區域。

Ulna 的**遠端**較小，其特徵為圓形的**頭部（head）**和 **ulnar styloid process（尺骨莖突）**。頭部的 anterolateral 和遠端部分覆蓋有關節軟骨。Ulnar styloid process 起於 ulna 的 posteromedial 方面，向遠端突出。

> **臨床重點**：橈骨與尺骨骨折
>
> Radius 和 ulna 近端透過複雜的韌帶系列附著於肱骨，遠端附著於腕骨。雖然骨骼是分开的，但它們作為一個整體功能。當前臂發生嚴重損傷時，通常涉及兩塊骨骼，導致兩骨骨折或更常見的是一骨骨折伴另一骨脫臼。
>
> 有三種經典的前臂損傷：
> - **Monteggia's fracture（Monteggia 骨折）**：ulna 近端三分之一骨折，合併 radius 頭部在肘部的**前方脫臼**
> - **Galeazzi's fracture（Galeazzi 骨折）**：radius 遠端三分之一骨折，合併手腕處 ulna 頭部的**半脫臼（subluxation）**
> - **Colles' fracture（Colles 骨折）**：radius 遠端骨折，合併**向後移位**
>
> 任何 radius 或 ulna 骨折都應拍攝肘部和手腕的額外影像，以排除脫臼。

---

## Distal Radio-Ulnar Joint（遠端橈尺關節）

遠端 radio-ulnar 關節位於 ulna 頭部的關節面、radius 末端的 **ulnar notch（尺骨切跡）** 以及**纖維軟骨關節盤（articular disc）** 之間。關節盤將 radio-ulnar 關節與腕關節分開。

三角形的 **articular disc（關節盤）** 由其 apex 附著於 ulna 上 styloid process 和頭部關節面之間的粗糙凹陷處，base 則附著於 radius 上 ulnar notch 和腕骨關節面之間的 angular margin。

遠端 radio-ulnar 關節允許 radius 的遠端在 ulna 上向前內側移動。

---

## Interosseous Membrane（骨間膜）

**Interosseous membrane（骨間膜）** 是一層薄的纖維膜，連接 radius 和 ulna 分別的內側和外側邊界。膜內的膠原蛋白纖維主要從 radius 斜向下方至 ulna。

骨間膜有一個遊離的上緣，位於 radial tuberosity 正下方；在其遠端三分之一處有一個小圓形孔隙。血管在 upper margin 上方在 anterior 和 posterior compartments 之間通行，並穿過下方的孔隙。

骨間膜連接 radius 和 ulna，且不妨礙 pronation 和 supination，並為前臂前側和後側 compartments 的肌肉提供附著處。膜內纖維的排列方向也與其將力量從 radius 轉移至 ulna、最終從手轉移至肱骨的功能一致。

---

## Pronation 與 Supination（旋前與旋後）

手的 **pronation** 和 **supination** 完全發生在前臂，涉及 radius 在肘部的旋轉和 radius 遠端在 ulna 上的移動。

在肘部，radial head 的上關節面在 **capitulum** 上旋轉，同時頭部側面的關節面在 ulna 的 **radial notch** 及關節囊的相鄰區域和 **anular ligament of the radius** 上滑動。在遠端 radio-ulnar 關節，radius 的 **ulnar notch** 在 ulna 頭部的凸面上向前滑動。

在這些運動中，骨骼由以下結構固定在一起：

- **近端 radio-ulnar 關節**的 **anular ligament of the radius**
- 沿 radius 和 ulna 全長的 **interosseous membrane**
- **遠端 radio-ulnar 關節**的 **articular disc**

因為手主要與 radius 關節，radius 遠端在 ulna 上向內側的移位使手從掌心向前（supinated）位置移動到掌心向後（pronated）位置。

### 參與 Pronation 與 Supination 的肌肉

**Supinator（旋後肌）**：位於前臂後側 compartment，源於 ulna 的 **supinator crest** 和肱骨的 **lateral epicondyle**，以及與肘關節相關的韌帶。Supinator 肌肉繞過 radius 上三分之一的后侧面和外侧面，附著於骨幹，位置在 oblique line 上方。當手處於 pronated 位置時，**biceps brachii** 和 **supinator** 的肌腱都繞過 radius 近端。當它們收縮時，會從骨骼上展開，產生手的 supination。

**Pronator teres 與 Pronator quadratus**：Pronation 來自 **pronator teres** 和 **pronator quadratus** 肌肉的作用。這兩塊肌肉都位於前臂前側 compartment：

- **Pronator teres**：從肱骨的 **medial epicondyle** 延伸至 radius 中段附近的 lateral surface
- **Pronator quadratus**：在 radius 和 ulna 遠端的前表面之間延伸

當這些肌肉收縮時，它們拉動 radius 遠端越過 ulna，產生手的 pronation。

**Anconeus**：除了肘關節的鉸鏈式屈伸外，還發生 ulna 遠端的一些外展，維持手的掌心在 pronation 期間圍繞中心軸的位置。參與此運動的肌肉是 **anconeus muscle**，這是前臂後側 compartment 中的一塊三角形肌肉，從 **lateral epicondyle** 延伸至 ulna 近端的外側 surface。

---

## 前臂前側 Compartment（Anterior Compartment）的肌肉

前臂前側（屈肌）compartment 的肌肉分為三層：**淺層（superficial）**、**中層（intermediate）** 和 **深層（deep）**。這些肌肉通常與以下功能相關：

- 腕關節的運動
- 手指（包括拇指）的屈曲
- Pronation

前臂前側 compartment 的所有肌肉都由 **median nerve** 神經支配，但 **flexor carpi ulnaris** 和 **flexor digitorum profundus** 的內側半由 **ulnar nerve** 神經支配。

### 淺層肌肉（Superficial Layer）

淺層的四塊肌肉——**flexor carpi ulnaris**、**palmaris longus**、**flexor carpi radialis** 和 **pronator teres**——都源自肱骨的 **medial epicondyle**，除 pronator teres 外，都從前臂延伸至手部。

#### Flexor Carpi Ulnaris（尺側腕屈肌）

**Flexor carpi ulnaris** 是淺層屈肌中最內側的肌肉，除了源自肱骨的 medial epicondyle 外，還從 olecranon 和 ulna 的後 border 有很長的線性起始。

**Ulnar nerve** 通過 **flexor carpi ulnaris** 的 humeral head 和 ulnar head 之間的三角形間隙進入前臂前側 compartment。肌肉纖維匯聚成一條肌腱，向遠端延伸並附著於手腕的 **pisiform bone（豌豆骨）**。從此處，力透過 **pisohamate ligament** 和 **pisometacarpal ligament** 傳遞至手腕的 **hamate bone（鉤骨）** 和 **metacarpal V（第五掌骨）** 的底部。

Flexor carpi ulnaris 是手腕有力的屈肌和內收肌，由 **ulnar nerve** 神經支配。

#### Palmaris Longus（掌長肌）

**Palmaris longus** 約存在於 85% 的人群中，介於 flexor carpi ulnaris 和 flexor carpi radialis 之間。這是一塊紡錘形肌肉，具有一條長肌腱，伸入手部並附著於 **flexor retinaculum（屈肌支持帶）** 和深層筋膜的厚層——**palmar aponeurosis（掌腱膜）**，位於手掌和手指皮膚的下方並與之附著。

除了作為手腕關節的輔助屈肌外，palmaris longus 還在握持時抵抗手掌皮膚的剪切力。

#### Flexor Carpi Radialis（橈側腕屈肌）

**Flexor carpi radialis** 位於 palmaris longus 的外側，在前臂遠端有一條大而明顯的肌腱。與形成前臂遠端內側邊緣的 flexor carpi ulnaris 肌腱不同，flexor carpi radialis 的肌腱位於正中線的外側。在此位置，肌腱容易觸診，成為尋找位於其外側的 **radial artery（橈動脈）** 脈搏的重要標誌。

Flexor carpi radialis 的肌腱穿過手腕外側由骨和筋膜形成的 compartment，附著於 **metacarpals II 和 III** 底部的 anterior surfaces。Flexor carpi radialis 是手腕有力的屈肌，也能使手腕外展。

#### Pronator Teres（旋前圓肌）

**Pronator teres** 源自肱骨的 medial epicondyle 和 **supraepicondylar ridge**，以及 ulna **coronoid process** 內側邊緣的一小線性區域。Median nerve 常通過此肌肉的 humeral head 和 ulnar head 之間離開 cubital fossa。Pronator teres 跨越前臂，附著於 radius 中段附近的 lateral surface 的橢圓形粗糙區。

Pronator teres 形成 **cubital fossa（肘窩）** 的內側邊界，在 pronation 期間使 radius 繞 ulna 旋轉。

| 肌肉 | 起始 | 附著 | 神經支配 | 功能 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Flexor carpi ulnaris** | 肱骨頭—肱骨 medial epicondyle；尺骨頭—olecranon 和 ulna 後 border | Pisiform bone，經 pisohamate 和 pisometacarpal ligaments 至 hamate 和 metacarpal V 底部 | **Ulnar nerve** (C7, C8, T1) | 屈曲和內收腕關節 |
| **Palmaris longus** | 肱骨 medial epicondyle | 手掌的 palmar aponeurosis | **Median nerve** (C7, C8) | 屈腕；抵抗握持時的剪切力 |
| **Flexor carpi radialis** | 肱骨 medial epicondyle | Metacarpals II 和 III 底部 | **Median nerve** (C6, C7) | 屈曲和外展腕關節 |
| **Pronator teres** | 肱骨頭—medial epicondyle 和相鄰 supra-epicondylar ridge；尺骨頭—coronoid process 內側 | Radius 中段外側面的粗糙區 | **Median nerve** (C6, C7) | Pronation |

### 中層肌肉（Intermediate Layer）

#### Flexor Digitorum Superficialis（淺指屈肌）

前臂前側 compartment 中層的肌肉是 **flexor digitorum superficialis**。這塊大肌肉有兩個 head：

- **Humero-ulnar head**：主要源自肱骨的 medial epicondyle 和 ulna coronoid process 相鄰的內側邊緣
- **Radial head**：源自 radius 的 anterior oblique line

**Median nerve** 和 **ulnar artery** 從兩個 heads 之間深層通過 flexor digitorum superficialis。

在遠端前臂，flexor digitorum superficialis 形成四條肌腱，穿過手腕的 **carpal tunnel** 進入四根手指。環指和中指的肌腱位於食指和小指肌腱的淺層。

在前臂、carpal tunnel 和四根手指的近端區域，flexor digitorum superficialis 的肌腱位於 **flexor digitorum profundus** 肌腱的前方。

在每根手指近端指骨底部附近，flexor digitorum superficialis 的肌腱分叉成兩部分，繞過 flexor digitorum profundus 肌腱的兩側，最終附著於**中指骨（middle phalanx）**的邊緣。

Flexor digitorum superficialis 屈曲每根手指的**掌指關節（metacarpophalangeal joint）**和**近端指間關節（proximal interphalangeal joint）**；也能屈曲腕關節。

| 肌肉 | 起始 | 附著 | 神經支配 | 功能 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Flexor digitorum superficialis** | Humero-ulnar head—肱骨 medial epicondyle 和 coronoid process 相鄰邊緣；Radial head—radius oblique line | 四條肌腱，附著於食指、中指、環指和小指中指骨的掌側面 | **Median nerve** (C8, T1) | 屈曲食指、中指、環指和小指的近端指間關節；也能屈曲相同手指的掌指關節和腕關節 |

# Gray's Anatomy — Upper Limb
## 前臂（前腔室）深層肌肉

### Flexor Digitorum Profundus（指深屈肌）

前臂前腔室有**三塊深層肌肉**：**flexor digitorum profundus**、**flexor pollicis longus**、和 **pronator quadratus**（圖 7.85）。

**Flexor digitorum profundus** 肌肉起點為尺骨的**前面及內側面**，以及**骨間膜前面**的內側半部（圖 7.85）。它發出四條肌腱，經過腕隧道進入四個內側手指。在大部分行程中，這些肌腱都位於 **flexor digitorum superficialis** 肌肉肌腱的深層。

在每根手指的**proximal phalanx（近節指骨）**的對側，每條 **flexor digitorum profundus** 的肌腱穿過覆蓋其上的 **flexor digitorum superficialis** 肌腱形成的裂隙，然後遠行，附著於**遠節指骨底部的前面**。

在手掌中，**lumbrical muscles（小魚際肌）**起點為 **flexor digitorum profundus** 肌腱的兩側（見圖 7.104）。

**Flexor digitorum profundus** 的內側半和外側半的神經支配如下：

- **外側半**（與食指和中指相關）由**正中神經的前骨間分支**（anterior interosseous nerve）支配。
- **內側半**（與無名指和小指相關）由**尺神經**支配。

**Flexor digitorum profundus** 可屈曲四指 的**metacarpophalangeal joints（掌指關節）**、**proximal interphalangeal joints（近端指間關節）**和**distal interphalangeal joints（遠端指間關節）**。由於肌腱跨越腕關節，它也能屈曲腕關節（表 7.12）。

> **臨床重點**：**Lateral half（外側半）**由正中神經（anterior interosseous nerve）支配，**medial half（內側半）**由尺神經支配。這解釋了為何正中神經損傷時食指和中指屈曲力量減弱，而尺神經損傷時無名指和小指受影響。

### Flexor Pollicis Longus（拇指長屈肌）

**Flexor pollicis longus** 肌肉起點為**橈骨前面**及**骨間膜前面**的橈側半（圖 7.85）。它是一塊強大的肌肉，形成一條粗大的肌腱，經過腕隧道，位於 **flexor digitorum superficialis** 和 **flexor digitorum profundus** 肌肉肌腱的外側，進入拇指並附著於**遠節指骨底部**。

**Flexor pollicis longus** 可屈曲拇指，由**正中神經的前骨間分支**支配（表 7.12）。

### Pronator Quadratus（旋前方肌）

**Pronator quadratus** 肌肉是遠端前臂中一塊扁平的方形肌肉（圖 7.85）。它起點為尺骨下端前面的線性脊，向外側橫行，附著於**橈骨前面**的扁平表面。它位於 **flexor digitorum profundus** 和 **flexor pollicis longus** 肌肉肌腱的深層，並被這些肌腱跨越。

**Pronator quadratus** 肌肉在旋前時將橈骨遠端向前拉過尺骨，由**正中神經的前骨間分支**支配（表 7.12）。

---

### 表 7.12 — 前臂前腔室深層肌肉

| 肌肉 | 起點 | 附著點 | 神經支配 | 功能 |
|------|------|--------|----------|------|
| **Flexor digitorum profundus** | 尺骨前面及內側面、骨間膜內側半 | 四條肌腱附著於食指、中指、無名指、小指的遠節指骨底部的掌側表面 | 外側半：正中神經（anterior interosseous nerve）；內側半：尺神經（C8, T1） | 屈曲食指、中指、無名指、小指的遠端指間關節；也能屈曲同指的掌指關節及腕關節 |
| **Flexor pollicis longus** | 橈骨前面、骨間膜橈側半 | 拇指遠節指骨底的掌側表面 | 正中神經（前骨間分支）（C7, C8） | 屈曲拇指的指間關節；也能屈曲拇指的掌指關節 |
